### 2026-11-17T17:53:42Z [**nyakang/nyaterm**](https://github.com/nyakang/nyaterm)

```
## NyaTerm v2.0.6  本次正式版本重点完善终端命令与路径补全、连接和启动配置，并改进串口设备选择、Android 屏幕操作与 MCP 命令执行体验。同时修复终端清屏后回滚显示、命令历史、数据目录迁移、云同步以及多项文件管理和界面问题。  ### ✨ 新增功能  - **命令路径补全与自定义规范**：命令建议现在可补全受支持 Shell 环境中的文件和目录路径，补充常见服务器命令及参数候选，并可从配置目录加载用户自定义的 JSON 补全规范。历史、命令及参数建议可先行展示，路径结果随后补充；对远程目录读取设置超时及数量限制。（PR #1251，#1028） - **可切换应用数据目录**：在设置中选择新的数据存储位置，支持迁移或采用已有数据，并增加重启、目录校验和中断后恢复处理，尽量避免迁移中途留下不可用的数据库。（PR #1243） - **连接默认参数**：可分别为各连接协议保存新建会话的默认设置，减少重复配置；现有连接仍保留自己的设置。（PR #1243） - **自定义启动会话**：启动行为支持“不恢复”“恢复上次会话”或“打开指定连接 / 连接分组”，可设置多个目标及其顺序；不支持无人值守启动的连接会受到限制。 - **串口连接时选择设备与波特率**：已保存的串口连接可配置为连接时询问设备端口、波特率或两者，便于应对 USB 串口设备重新插拔后端口变化；会话标题跟随实际设备。（PR #1289） - **连接管理与共享**：支持复制或分享已保存连接的摘要信息，连接列表增加标签搜索，并改善连接和分组的拖放排序、层级移动与拖动反馈。 - **网络监控**：扩展网络使用历史、逐网卡流量查看和相关筛选交互。 - **MCP 终端执行策略**：增加自动、会话内和后台执行模式配置；MCP 的 `terminal_start` 在适用的后台 SSH 执行场景下可有限等待命令结果，减少短命令反复轮询。（PR #1286）  ### 🐛 问题修复  - **终端清屏与回滚**：修复 Ctrl+L 清屏并继续输出后，回滚时可能出现旧提示符或缺少最新输出的问题，改进缓存快照的新鲜度检查。（PR #1295） - **命令历史与补全**：修复 Tab 补全后的命令未正确收录到历史、重复历史记录，以及已提交命令又成为下一次输入等问题；对嵌套 Shell 和上下文不足的情况避免给出错误路径建议。（PR #1279、PR #1281） - **折叠输出与终端显示**：修复命令输出折叠后，高亮、建议弹窗定位和滚动显示可能异常的问题；修正终端横向滚动条预留空间、活动主题颜色查询等细节。（PR #1265） - **Shell 与路径兼容性**：改进终端路径识别、引用和特殊字符转义，修复部分 zsh 注入清理及命令确认场景。（PR #1264，#1288） - **云同步与数据恢复**：对适用的 S3 读取和列举失败增加有限重试，改进冲突合并、同步完成反馈及迁移恢复过程；处理 macOS 数据库迁移涉及的文件系统与锁定兼容问题。（PR #1243、PR #1270、PR #1272） - **文件和资产操作**：修复批量选择资产后的菜单操作只作用于单个项目的问题，增强批量删除的一致性；改进文件剪贴板传输、文件图标及拖放完成时的状态处理。（PR #1271） - **MCP 连接稳定性**：修复桥接协商尚未完成就请求工具列表、重新连接后发现信息过期、恢复会话不完整等情况，并完善连接身份和等待超时处理。（PR #1269、PR #1283） - **AI 对话草稿**：修复 Agent 运行完成后，用户预先输入的后续消息草稿可能丢失的问题。（PR #1253）  ### ⚡ 性能与稳定性优化  - **Android 屏幕显示**：更新屏幕控制工具栏，完善返回、主页、最近任务、适配显示与粘贴等操作，并引入 GPU 平面图像渲染路径以改善屏幕数据处理。 - **终端补全资源控制**：限制本地和 SFTP 路径枚举、过期任务及缓存占用，避免目录响应迟缓时阻塞建议更新；折叠输出时优化高亮复用与计算范围。（PR #1251） - **远程桌面渲染**：减少不可见窗口的无效纹理上传，并优化较窄脏矩形的上传处理。（PR #1263） - **运行与持久化可靠性**：加强会话恢复、连接重试和数据目录迁移的边界处理，减少失败后的错误状态残留。  ### ♻️ 重构与优化  - 拆分终端补全运行时、连接树管理和部分桌面服务，统一状态与取消机制；这些主要是维护性调整，不代表功能模块整体替换。  ### 🎨 界面与交互优化  - **容量与速率单位**：修正按 1024 换算的内存、磁盘、文件大小、网络速率等数值的显示单位，统一使用 KiB / MiB / GiB 等二进制单位；不改变实际数值或保存格式。（PR #1287） - **连接与弹窗**：改善连接列表拖拽指示、设备选择和子窗口在全屏或最大化父窗口中的居中定位。（PR #1285） - **悬停卡片**：改善靠近屏幕边界时的横向定位，减少卡片超出可用区域的情况。（#1170） - **资源监控面板**：调整网络卡片布局、折叠及顶部硬件信息分页，避免较长单位标签被裁切。  ### 📚 文档与国际化  - 补充路径补全的支持范围与限制说明，完善串口选择、设置选项、Android 屏幕操作及容量单位相关的多语言文案。  ### 👥 贡献者  - @WerVa：完善命令与文件路径补全、补全目录及相关稳定性保护。（PR #1251） - @fonaix：实现数据目录切换与连接默认值、改进串口连接配置，并修复子窗口定位问题。（PR #1243、PR #1289、PR #1285） - @zhangzp0127：改进 MCP 工具发现、重连恢复与命令启动后的有限等待。（PR #1269、PR #1283、PR #1286） - @Jensen-Yao：修复 Tab 补全历史、重复记录、折叠输出定位及 zsh 命令清理问题。（PR #1264、PR #1265、PR #1279、PR #1281） - @HarpeLm：修复资产批量操作及 macOS 数据目录迁移兼容性，并补充跨平台测试。（PR #1270、PR #1271、PR #1272、PR #1274、PR #1275、PR #1282） - @LunaSpica：修正容量与网络速率的二进制单位显示。（PR #1287） - @dxianping：减少远程桌面未显示窗口的无效纹理上传。（PR #1263） - @shallFun4Learning：修复 AI Agent 运行后的后续输入草稿保留问题。（PR #1253） - @zhonghes：修复终端清屏后缓存滚动快照失效引发的旧内容显示问题。（PR #1295）  ### ⚠️ 升级说明  - 切换应用数据目录会涉及数据校验与应用重启；请先确认目标目录可读写，迁移期间不要手动删除原目录。 - 对串口连接启用“连接时询问”后，交互式启动可选择端口或波特率；无人值守恢复及自动化启动不会弹出选择框，建议使用固定参数的连接。 
```

```
## 改进  - **kn：执行中输入的消息在当前工具调用结束后插入本轮**（#212, #213）。以前要等整轮结束，才作为新的一轮发送。   - 消息先进入队列，发出前仍可编辑、移动、删除。   - 当前这批工具调用一结束，就插入本轮，在对话里显示为 `(steered)`，模型在工具结果之后马上就能读到。   - 斜杠命令仍等本轮结束；模型直接回答结束、没有再调用工具时，队列照旧作为下一轮发送。   - 这一轮在读取消息前结束（例如被中断）时，消息退回队列，不会丢。
```

```
# Pebrel 2.5.9  ## English  macOS, Linux, and Android downloads are Preview builds.  ### Added  - Added an option to keep the local directory or SSH host in tab titles. The current AI task remains visible on hover. - Added the option to delete selected files, symbolic links, or empty folders from the SFTP file drawer after confirmation. - Added manual sleep and optional automatic sleep for idle local tabs. Waking a tab starts a fresh shell in its previous directory and restores recent terminal output.  ### Fixed  - Fixed macOS agent hook connections closing before a delayed message arrived. - Fixed tab groups filling the entire row on hover. The title and guide line now highlight. - Fixed low contrast in the Linux Settings navigation when the window background is translucent. - Fixed image paste staying blocked after a Linux pane reached its 32-image quota and the earlier work had finished. - Fixed the About page showing Windows on other desktop platforms. - Fixed wrapped formulas losing alignment when partly scrolled out of view and the terminal still has their source lines. Columns stay in place, and gaps no longer activate nearby links. - Fixed local file links broken by UTF-8 truncation when the original path can be recovered from the link. - Fixed terminal stalls when saving directory history. Recent directories remain available in memory if the history file cannot be locked. - Fixed Antigravity subagents taking over the foreground session or marking its turn complete. - Fixed wide terminal glyphs overlapping the next cell.  ### Improved  - Improved desktop text rendering with grayscale antialiasing by default. Use `pebrel.luau` to choose system or subpixel antialiasing. Subpixel mode requires an opaque window and platform support.  ## 中文  macOS、Linux 和 Android 下载为预览版。  ### 新增  - 新增标签标题选项，可保留本地目录名或 SSH 主机名。悬停标签仍可查看当前 AI 任务。 - 新增 SFTP 文件抽屉的删除操作，确认后可删除所选文件、符号链接和空目录。 - 新增空闲本地标签的手动休眠和可选的自动休眠。唤醒时在原目录启动新的 Shell，并恢复近期终端输出。  ### 修复  - 修复 macOS 上助手 Hook 连接在延迟消息到达前就被关闭的问题。 - 修复鼠标悬停时标签分组整行被填色的问题，现在只高亮标题和引导线。 - 修复 Linux 窗口背景半透明时设置导航文字对比度过低的问题。 - 修复 Linux 同一窗格粘贴 32 张图片后，即使之前的任务已结束也无法继续粘贴的问题。 - 修复其他桌面平台的关于页误显示 Windows 的问题。 - 修复终端仍保留公式源行时，换行公式部分滚出视区后的错位。固定列保持原位，点击空白不会误开邻近链接。 - 修复因 UTF-8 截断而失效的本地文件链接，可在路径能够从链接中恢复时打开原文件。 - 修复保存目录历史时终端卡顿的问题。无法锁定历史文件时，仍可使用内存中的近期目录。 - 修复 Antigravity 子会话接管前台会话或将前台任务误标为完成的问题。 - 修复终端宽字形覆盖相邻单元格的问题。  ### 改进  - 改进桌面文字的抗锯齿，默认使用灰度模式，可在 `pebrel.luau` 中选择系统或子像素模式。子像素模式需要平台支持，且窗口须为不透明。  ## Contributors  <p>   <a href="https://github.com/Kuddev"><img src="https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/292709043?v=4&amp;s=96" width="48" height="48" alt="Kuddev" title="Kuddev" /></a>   <a href="https://github.com/whitoud"><img src="https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/54259693?v=4&amp;s=96" width="48" height="48" alt="whitoud" title="whitoud" /></a>   <a href="https://github.com/Cec1c"><img src="https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/170788854?v=4&amp;s=96" width="48" height="48" alt="Cec1c" title="Cec1c" /></a>   <a href="https://github.com/not-exist"><img src="https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/121529946?v=4&amp;s=96" width="48" height="48" alt="not-exist" title="not-exist" /></a>   <a href="https://github.com/onlynet"><img src="https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/21923781?v=4&amp;s=96" width="48" height="48" alt="onlynet" title="onlynet" /></a>   <a href="https://github.com/Sakyvo"><img src="https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/169263722?v=4&amp;s=96" width="48" height="48" alt="Sakyvo" title="Sakyvo" /></a> </p>  - # 中文  本次 Next 更新将上游升级至 DeepSeek Harness v0.2.2-rc.3，换用全新瓷青“鲸尾”应用与托盘图标，带来实验性「DeepSeek 会话导入」、多层级 AgentTeam 协作与 DeepSeek Search API 默认搜索，新增 Host 启动超时配置、慢启动插件提示与渲染性能采样，并将 Windows 应用内更新升级为原子目录替换，同时修复 macOS 滚动区窗口拖动、Linux AppImage 沙箱与 Windows 权限传播等问题。  DSH NEXT 是社区维护的开源项目，并非 DeepSeek 官方产品。  **上游版本：** [DeepSeek Harness v0.2.2-rc.3](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/releases/tag/dsh-v0.2.2-rc.3)。  ## 新增与改进  - **全新瓷青“鲸尾”图标:** 应用主图标与系统托盘图标升级为瓷青“鲸尾”设计，适配 macOS 默认、深色与色调外观；优化 Windows 暗色模式下的窗口初始背景，减少启动白闪。 - **上游 `0.2.2-rc.2` / `rc.3` 新特性:** 新增实验性「DeepSeek 会话导入」插件，支持将 DeepSeek App 或网页版历史会话导入工作区继续对话；支持 Git Worktree 按分支／提交审阅改动与 GitHub 评审、多层级具名子代理及按会话开启的 AgentTeam 协作；网页搜索默认改用 DeepSeek Search API 并支持分别选择搜索与抓取计费方式；支持侧栏文件搜索、向上滚动自动加载历史消息及 Office 原生工具。 - **Host 启动超时与诊断增强:** 桌面设置与恢复助手支持设置 Host 启动超时时间（60 至 600 秒或无限制），超时 1 分钟后通过系统通知列出仍在加载的插件；诊断导出与故障报告新增渲染进程动画、帧率与长任务采样，并优化恢复助手的长文本滚动与日志展示。 - **版本隔离与内置组件调整:** 优先使用安装包随附的核心组件解析版本，防止旧 Profile 中的残留依赖引发启动报错；启动前自动备份并清理退役的旧版团队协作与工作树插件配置；可选 `macOS Computer Use` 组件恢复随安装包提供，内置 `dshmarket` 更新至 `1.73.19`。  ## 修复  - **Windows 更新可靠性与沙箱权限:** 将 Windows 应用内更新改为临时目录解压校验后原子替换（支持文件占用重试与失败回滚），防止安装失败损坏现有应用；修复受限沙箱（`workspace-write`）对部分子目录权限传播遗漏导致无法写入的问题。 - **Linux 沙箱与会话菜单:** 修复 Linux AppImage 在受限沙箱下无法调用自身运行器导致沙箱工具不可用的问题；修复会话原工作目录不存在时 `/` 菜单丢失技能列表的问题。 - **macOS 窗口拖动与 Host 稳定性:** 修复 macOS 下聊天记录、侧栏或插件页滚动后顶部区域无法拖动窗口的问题；修复无代理环境下直连网络因底层连接提前关闭导致 Plugin Host 异常退出的问题。  ## 下载  从本页 **Assets** 下载对应平台的安装包：  | 平台 | 文件 | | --- | --- | | macOS Universal · Apple Silicon / Intel | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.23-next-universal.dmg` | | Windows x64 | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.23-next-x64-Setup.exe` | | Linux x64 · AppImage | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.23-next-x86_64.AppImage` | | Linux x64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.23-next-amd64.deb` | | Linux x64 · tar.gz | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.23-next-linux-x64.tar.gz` | | Linux ARM64 · AppImage | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.23-next-arm64.AppImage` | | Linux ARM64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.23-next-arm64.deb` | | Linux ARM64 · tar.gz | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.23-next-linux-arm64.tar.gz` |  可使用本页 `SHA256SUMS.txt` 核验全部安装包。  ## 使用提示  - **macOS 后续分包预告:** 后续版本起 macOS 将改为分别提供 Apple Silicon（ARM64）与 Intel（x64）独立安装包，不再提供双架构通用包以减小体积；应用内更新将自动匹配本机芯片架构，手动下载时请注意选择对应版本。 - 请先退出旧版应用并备份 `~/.dsh`（或在“设置 → 桌面设置”中使用“备份”），安装后验证常用插件和工作流程。 - 如需通过应用内终端管理 `desktop` Profile 的插件，请先启动 Desktop 完成该 Profile 的初始化。 - 从 2.0.17-next 或更早版本升级时，建议手动下载安装；旧版应用内更新可能无法完成下载。 - Office 文档技能首次使用时将随包运行时安装到 `~/.dsh/dsh-runtimes/dsh-next-primary-runtime`，需要额外磁盘空间。 - 默认与旧稳定版共用 `~/.dsh`；Profile 不隔离同一目录中的会话、账号和凭据。 - 可在“设置 → 桌面设置”或恢复助手中切换数据目录，也可通过绝对路径 `DSH_DESKTOP_NEXT_HOME` 隔离 DSH 数据（应用内选择优先于环境变量），但不会因此隔离 AA 的默认共享登录与设备绑定状态。  [完整变更记录](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop/compare/v2.0.22-next...v2.0.23-next) · [项目网站](https://dshdesktop.cn)  DSH NEXT 已取代原稳定版通道。  ---  # English  This Next release upgrades upstream to DeepSeek Harness v0.2.2-rc.3, introduces the new Porcelain whale-tail app and tray icons, adds the experimental DeepSeek chat import plugin, multi-level AgentTeam collaboration, and default DeepSeek Search API integration, adds a configurable Host startup limit with slow-start plugin notices and renderer drawing diagnostics, upgrades Windows in-app updates to atomic directory replacement, and fixes macOS drag regions over scrolled content, Linux AppImage sandboxing, and Windows ACL propagation.  DSH NEXT is a community-maintained open-source project, not an official DeepSeek product.  **Upstream version:** [DeepSeek Harness v0.2.2-rc.3](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/releases/tag/dsh-v0.2.2-rc.3).  ## New and improved  - **New Porcelain whale-tail icons:** Updated the app and tray icons to the Porcelain whale-tail design with support for macOS Default, Dark, and Tinted appearances; adopted an initial dark-mode window background on Windows to reduce startup white flashes. - **Upstream `0.2.2-rc.2` / `rc.3` highlights:** Added the experimental DeepSeek chat import plugin to bring past DeepSeek App or web conversations into a workspace; added branch/commit diff and GitHub review support in Git Worktrees, named multi-level subagents with per-session AgentTeam collaboration, default DeepSeek Search API with separate Search/Fetch billing choices, sidebar file search, automatic loading of earlier chat history on scroll, and native Office tools. - **Host startup limit and diagnostics:** Added configurable Host startup timeouts (60–600 s or unlimited) in Desktop Settings and Quick Recovery with a 1-minute system notification naming plugins still loading; diagnostics exports and failure reports now sample renderer animations, frame rate, and long tasks, with scrollable long text and refined error summaries in the recovery assistant. - **Version isolation and bundled components:** Prioritized the app's bundled core packages when resolving Profiles to prevent stale dependencies from breaking startup; automatically backed up and migrated retired legacy worktree and team bundles before launch; restored the optional `macOS Computer Use` package inside the bundle and updated `dshmarket` to `1.73.19`.  ## Fixes  - **Windows updater reliability and sandbox permissions:** Upgraded Windows in-app updates to extract into a staging folder and swap installation directories atomically with lock retries and rollback so interrupted updates do not leave a broken installation; backfilled missed Windows ACL integrity labels inside `workspace-write` folders. - **Linux sandboxing and skill menu:** Kept the Linux AppImage runner accessible inside the `workspace-write` sandbox so confined tools remain usable; kept skills listed in the `/` menu even when a session's recorded folder no longer exists. - **macOS window dragging and Host stability:** Fixed scrolled conversation, sidebar, and plugin content blocking window dragging on top chrome bars on macOS; installed the bundled HTTP dispatcher for direct outbound connections to prevent premature socket closes from terminating an idle Plugin Host.  ## Downloads  Choose the matching installer from **Assets** below:  | Platform | File | | --- | --- | | macOS Universal · Apple Silicon / Intel | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.23-next-universal.dmg` | | Windows x64 | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.23-next-x64-Setup.exe` | | Linux x64 · AppImage | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.23-next-x86_64.AppImage` | | Linux x64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.23-next-amd64.deb` | | Linux x64 · tar.gz | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.23-next-linux-x64.tar.gz` | | Linux ARM64 · AppImage | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.23-next-arm64.AppImage` | | Linux ARM64 · Debian / Ubuntu | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.23-next-arm64.deb` | | Linux ARM64 · tar.gz | `DSH-NEXT-2.0.23-next-linux-arm64.tar.gz` |  Use `SHA256SUMS.txt` from this release to verify all installers.  ## Usage notes  - **Upcoming split macOS installers:** Starting in upcoming releases, macOS builds will ship as separate Apple Silicon (ARM64) and Intel (x64) packages instead of a Universal DMG to reduce download size. In-app updates will match your Mac's architecture automatically; when downloading manually, choose the package for your chip. - Quit the previous version and back up `~/.dsh` (or use **Back up** in **Settings → Desktop settings**) before installing. Verify your usual plugins and workflows afterward. - Before managing plugins for the `desktop` Profile from the bundled terminal, launch Desktop to initialize that Profile. - When upgrading from 2.0.17-next or earlier, download and install manually; in-app updates in those versions may not complete the download. - The Office skills install the bundled runtime under `~/.dsh/dsh-runtimes/dsh-next-primary-runtime` on first use, requiring additional disk space. - Defaults to the shared `~/.dsh` home. Profiles do not isolate sessions, accounts, or credentials within that home. - Change the data directory in **Settings → Desktop settings** or the recovery assistant, or set `DSH_DESKTOP_NEXT_HOME` to an absolute path to isolate DSH data (an in-app choice takes precedence over the environment variable); this does not isolate AA’s default shared sign-in and device-binding state.  [Full changelog](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop/compare/v2.0.22-next...v2.0.23-next) · [Project website](https://dshdesktop.cn)  DSH NEXT replaces the former Stable edition channel. 
```

### 2026-11-14T14:12:23Z [**ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI**](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI)

```
自 v0.20.0 以来，dsh-TUI 跟上 DSH `0.2.2-rc.3` 验证线（继续兼容 `0.2.2-rc.2` 及更早验证线）， 让插件往输入框的 `@` 补全里加行，修好迁移在全新 profile 上找不到宿主框架包、Claude 内核在 SDK 未就绪时误报「未安装」，以及更新/退出时子进程 stderr 尾部引发的崩溃。  ## 亮点  - **适配 DSH `0.2.2-rc.3`** — 主适配线前移到 `0.2.2-rc.3`，peer 范围同时保留 `0.2.2-rc.2`、`0.2.2-rc.1` 等旧验证线，停在 rc.2 的用户也能直接装这版。rc.3 是版本列车重发：共有包的 `.d.ts` 零变化，唯一 `.js` 变化是 `dsh-tool-cordis` 的 API 目录，宿主复刻指纹与 patch surface 内容都没变，本版没有 TUI 可见行为改动。上游新增的 `@deepseek-ai/dsh-deepseek-chat-import` 是 web-only bundle（浏览器端导入 DeepSeek 官网对话导出），TUI 没有对应入口，刻意不接入。([#2009](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/2009) by @Lucas2011wastaken) - **插件可以往 `@` 补全里加行（实验性）** — 插件用 `ctx.tuiMentions.register({ id, title, complete(query, signal) })` 往输入框的 `@` 补全浮窗加行，与工作区文件共用一张卡：出现插件行时卡片标题从「文件」变成「提及」，插件行按与文件同一套模糊分并排（文件顺序不动、平分归文件）。行是 `{ label, detail?, insertText }`，选中后把纯文本 `insertText` 替换敲入的 `@query`，提交时不按路径解析、不当附件。每次查询单独一个 `AbortSignal`，继续打字即中止上一次，单次预算 300ms；每提供方每次 ≤50 行，每插件 ≤4 个、全局 ≤16 个。没有提供方时补全浮窗逐帧不变。类型 `TuiMentionProvider` / `TuiMentionItem` 进 `./extensions` 与 `./api`，规则表见[准入与开发指南](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/blob/main/tui-profile/docs/plugin-admission-and-development.md)接缝十五。([#1716](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1716) by @ccch1mneyyy)  ## 需要留意的行为变化  - **`@` 补全的插件行只做文本插入** — 选中插件行只替换当前 token，不参与文件提及展开器；`insertText` 限纯文本、单行、≤200 字符，含 `@` 提及记号的在准入时丢弃。`register` 对非法输入、重复 id 或超额直接抛错，dispose 随插件 activation 回收。这是实验性接缝，仍可能随 TUI Profile 演进调整；没有插件行时补全浮窗与之前逐帧一致。 - **Claude 内核行不再显示「未安装」** — SDK 未就绪但检测到 CLI 时，行显示 `claude-code vX.Y.Z · SDK 未就绪`（英文 `SDK unavailable`），Enter 仍打开 SDK 安装向导。SDK 仍是 Claude 后端的必要依赖，缺 SDK 时保持不可启动；CLI 路径查找、登录与模型调用逻辑不变。 - **迁移失败提示分流** — profile 内编译产物缺失或过旧仍是 `needs the profile's compiled copy`，提示先跑 `dsh-tui update`；入口存在但依赖加载或执行失败改报 `could not run` 与具体包名/原因，不再一律要求升级 TUI。 - **rc.3 与 `/update`** — rc.3 没有 TUI 可见变化；程序内 `/update` 会在目标 TUI 的 peer 范围内选已发布的最高版本，因此会把 dsh 引擎从 rc.1 / rc.2 对齐到 `0.2.2-rc.3`。  ## 修复  - **更新/退出不再被子进程 stderr 尾部打断** — 子进程 stderr reporter 销毁即进入终态：迟到尾部立即丢弃，不再创建定时器或调用通知，并清空只属于它的冷却表；此前这种尾部会重新调度通知、访问已失效 Channel 并以 exit 7 崩溃。活动期的去重/冷却与真实 lease 错误语义保持不变：未销毁 reporter 的负对照仍以 exit 7 暴露 `Channel UI lifetime has ended`。([#1981](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1981) by @ccch1mneyyy；诊断与补丁建议来自 @frederick-wang 在 [#1979](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/issues/1979) 的记录) - **Claude 内核检测** — 已检测到的 Claude Code CLI（包括 Homebrew 安装）在 SDK 不可用时仍显示版本，不再笼统标成「未安装」；CLI 版本仍只探测一次，没有 CLI 或取不到版本时不编造版本，SDK 缺失仍不可启动并保留安装向导。([#1978](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1978) by @ccch1mneyyy) - **迁移在全新 profile 上可用** — `dsh-tui migrate` 与 TUI 内 `/migrate` 的子进程在第一次导入迁移模块前安装宿主（PATH 上的 `dsh`）的框架包解析，冷 profile 不再因缺少 `@deepseek-ai/dsh-session-persistence-jsonl` 等 peer 而失败。解析只使用宿主公开的 `createRuntimeResolution` 与 `PluginPackages`，不调用会改写 manifest/root 的 `loadProfileDirectory` / `prepareProfile`，也不组合用户插件，成功与失败都会释放 Cordis 作用域；旧 profile 只有 `cli.js` 时仍直接执行。([#1768](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1768) by @FlameTN7) - **`@` 补全的候选与按键** — 接受时读取当前草稿与光标位置、校验当前 query 后再替换当前 token；异步返回期间保留用户当前选择，迟到结果不覆盖高亮；同一 stdin 批次的 Enter/Tab 不再用旧草稿或越界索引，未实际接受候选时继续走原有的实时草稿提交路径。([#1716](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1716) by @ccch1mneyyy)  ## 兼容与升级  - **DSH 主适配线**：`0.2.2-rc.3`；`0.2.2-rc.2`、`0.2.2-rc.1`、`0.2.1-rc.5`、`0.2.1-rc.4`、`0.2.1-rc.3`、`0.2.1-rc.2`、`0.2.0-rc.2`、`0.2.0-rc.1`、`0.1.7-rc.2`、`0.1.7-rc.1` 以及更早的验证线仍在契约内，契约外版本打漂移警告。 - **DSH 在 npm 上的 `latest` 仍是 `0.2.2-rc.1`**：`0.2.2-rc.3` 走 `next` 标签。程序内 `/update` 会在目标 TUI 的 peer 范围内选已发布的最高版本，因此会把 dsh 引擎对齐到 `0.2.2-rc.3`。 - **从 v0.20.0 升级**：程序内 `/update`（本版会一并把 dsh 引擎对齐到 `0.2.2-rc.3`、并对齐全局启动器），或   `npm install -g @deepseek-ai/dsh @deepseek-harness-tui/dsh-tui@0.20.1`。 - **`dst` 自动化 CLI 的验证线未同步前移**：仍验证到 `0.2.1-rc.5`；在 0.2.2 宿主上可用 `dst dsh` 原样转交官方参数。 - **嫡系插件清单**：随包的 `tui-profile/feed/first-party.json` 钉到 `0.20.1`。 - **DSH `0.2.2` 要求 Node `>=24.9`** — 本包自身仍声明 `^22.19 || >=24`；用更旧的 Node 运行 `0.2.2` 宿主属于上游未支持组合，升级前请先确认 `node -v`。 - **Windows 离线安装器**：引擎验证范围随契约前移到 `0.2.2-rc.3`；已装更高的 RC 会降回验证线。  ## 安装  sh npm install -g @deepseek-ai/dsh @deepseek-harness-tui/dsh-tui@0.20.1   偏好一键安装的用户可下载 Release 中的 `dsh-tui-setup.zip`（解压后 macOS / Linux 运行 `sh install.sh`，Windows 双击 `install.bat`） 或 Windows 离线安装程序 `dsh-tui-setup-win-x64.exe`；便携包解压即用。   <!-- dsh-tui:generated-notes --> <!-- Release notes generated using configuration in .github/release.yml at v0.20.1 -->  ## What's Changed * fix(migrate): resolve host peers for the migrate subprocess by @FlameTN7 in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1768 * feat(composer): admit plugin @-mention providers by @ccch1mneyyy in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1716 * fix(claude): distinguish unavailable SDK from installed CLI by @ccch1mneyyy in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1978 * fix(stderr): ignore late child output after reporter disposal by @ccch1mneyyy in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/1981 * chore(upstream): adapt to dsh 0.2.2-rc.3 by @Lucas2011wastaken in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/2009 * chore(release): prepare v0.20.1 by @Lucas2011wastaken in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/2012   **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/compare/v0.20.0...v0.20.1
```

```
[中文](#中文) | [English](#english)  ### 中文  OxideTerm 2.3.3 将会话管理器迁入侧边栏并重做紧凑主机监控，同时带来模板批量创建 SSH 连接、BLE 设备插件协议、受信任主机密钥管理、默认可编辑的多行粘贴预览、终端缩放与高吞吐渲染优化，以及跨平台字体和输入法修复。  #### 🗂️ 会话管理、工作台与导航  - **侧边栏会话管理器**：会话管理器现可直接作为侧边栏面板使用，在保持 SFTP 停靠于侧栏下方的同时与独立标签页互斥切换；面板支持本地搜索（Esc 关闭并重置输入）、整行拖拽排序，以及基于 Ctrl/Cmd 和 Shift 的多选操作与右键批量删除。（PR [#796](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/796)） - **分组统计修复**：会话管理器分组徽标现在将已保存的 BLE 设备配置纳入计数统计。（PR [#793](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/793)） - **模板批量添加 SSH 连接**：在会话管理器工具栏与命令行（`oxideterm connections batch-create`）中支持按 OpenSSH 风格模板搭配逐行主机清单批量生成连接，覆盖 `{host}`、`{profile}`、`{user}`、`{port}`、`{name}`、`{group}` 占位符与最多 2,000 条记录的预检。应用前会区分新增、更新、跳过与错误条目并采用整批原子写入（存在任何无效行即拒绝部分写入）；已保存模板写入系统钥匙串或加密便携保险库，并严格保留 `ProxyCommand` 的参数边界。（PR [#773](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/773)） - **紧凑主机监控侧栏**：重构连接监控面板，以更紧凑的信息布局集中呈现 CPU 使用率与走势、可按需展开的逐核负载网格、内存与交换空间、系统运行时间、磁盘容量与 I/O 速率、网络收发走势以及连接延迟。 - **精简活动会话树**：移除了活动会话面板中冗余的焦点钻取视图与面包屑导航，统一为树形视图与主机视图，并梳理节点、端口转发和终端编号展示，配合导轨线、分组状态指示与直达操作按钮提升浏览效率。 - **欢迎工作台与标签作用域**：优化欢迎页最近连接与快捷入口排版，在标题栏新增标签作用域选择器以按组快速筛选标签页，并精简设置页卡片外壳、规则编辑器及快捷命令发送栏布局。  #### ⚡ 终端体验、粘贴与编解码  - **多行粘贴默认可编辑**：开启粘贴保护时，多行粘贴会直接进入可编辑预览框，支持 Enter 换行、Esc 取消、撤销/重做与一键剥离 Markdown 代码块围栏；若未作修改，则沿用原样文本并保持原始换行交由终端粘贴处理。 - **禅模式边缘退出按钮**：处于禅模式时将鼠标移至窗口顶部边缘即可呼出退出按钮，无需记忆快捷键也能随时退出，同时避免顶部提示控件抢占终端输入焦点。（PR [#799](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/799)） - **终端缩放与高吞吐输出优化**：修复终端调整尺寸时网格短暂空白与提示符重绘不完整的问题；优化 PTY 缩放调度并减少高吞吐输出期间的重复文本快照、样式哈希、高亮扫描与流式链接识别分配，兼顾合并字符输入边界与流畅度。（PR [#784](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/784)） - **传统编码与分包 UTF-8 修复**：在 GBK、Big5、Shift_JIS、EUC-JP、EUC-KR、Windows-1252 等旧编码以及 Telnet 用户名输入场景中，无法编码的字符现改为发送 `?` 而非 HTML 数字实体；跨多次读取分包到达的合法 UTF-8 字符也不再触发误报编码切换提示。（PR [#776](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/776)、[#777](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/777)）  #### 🔐 SSH、主机密钥与 Shell 集成  - **受信任主机密钥管理**：在「设置 → 连接」中新增受信任主机（Known Hosts）管理界面，支持按主机、端口或指纹搜索、复制指纹、删除条目以及直接打开信任文件；当服务器公钥变更时，确认弹窗支持直接「替换密钥并连接」，且仅替换本次核对过主机、端口、密钥类型与预期指纹的对应条目。 - **ProxyJump 跳板路由修复**：拆分跳板节点的路由归属与连接身份，修复已保存的多跳 SSH 路由在恢复和复用时的匹配问题，保留每一跳独立的连接策略与上游代理策略。 - **远程 Shell 集成 v7**：仅在经会话校验的对应 SSH 通道内启用 OSC 133 命令标记与私有 OSC 钩子，同时即使服务端拒绝传递 `LC_OXIDETERM_SESSION` 也继续保留 OSC 7 工作目录追踪；已注入的 v6 集成脚本可平滑升级。 - **SFTP 与连接表单修复**：恢复独立 SFTP 表单的高级选项配置与页面滚动，使 SSH 隧道对话框复用统一的连接表单背景，并修复密钥导入弹窗及多行输入框无法滚动的问题。  #### 🔌 插件生态、BLE 与 MCP  - **BLE 设备插件接口、工具栏与浮窗**：新增 `ble.read` 与 `ble.write` 插件权限及 `ble.sessions`、`ble.describe`、`ble.read`、`ble.write`、`ble.onNotification`、`ble.offNotification` 宿主接口，支持通过插件配置声明终端工具栏三态功能按钮以及跟随活动终端窗格切换的浮动面板；GATT 负载不落盘、不写入日志或错误文本。由于 BLE 断线重连会分配新的终端会话 ID，插件需重新查询 `ble.sessions` 并重新订阅通知。（PR [#775](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/775)） - **MCP 终端历史分页工具**：新增独立于可见视口的 `terminals_history` 工具，支持 AI 助手基于快照槽位与游标（`next_cursor`）分页读取完整回滚缓冲区或当前屏幕，用户滚动终端窗口不会打断读取进度。（PR [#790](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/790)）  #### 🖥️ 界面统一、输入法与平台兼容  - **深色主题层次与菜单统一**：增强深色主题（含 One Dark）下侧边栏、标题栏等周边壳层与主工作区画布的明暗对比并补全折叠态边栏边框；统一终端、编辑器、IDE、SFTP、文件管理器、标签页、会话管理器及 SSH 算法编辑器的右键菜单和下拉菜单样式、图标行、快捷键对齐与分割线。 - **按钮反馈与知识库弹窗键盘交互**：保持实底工具栏按钮悬停时的独立色调及危险操作按钮悬停填充透明度一致；新建知识库文档弹窗支持在标题框直接按 Enter 提交，并将 Tab 焦点循环稳定锁定在弹窗内直至关闭。（PR [#781](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/781)、[#783](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/783)、[#785](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/785)、[#786](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/786)） - **标签栏触控板滚动与编辑器输入法修复**：标签栏横向滚动优先跟随触控板主滑动轴以减少斜向手势抖动；修复内置编辑器在输入法组字期间的光标位置、可见性、选区隔离与自动折行锚点错位问题。（PR [#780](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/780)、[#782](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/782)） - **GPUI 0.3.12 升级与三大平台底层修复**：升级内置 GPUI，恢复 Linux XIM 在异常复合文本编码下的字符提交避免输入丢字，修正 Windows DirectWrite 分段字体渲染时的字距与原生 Windows 路径下的本地项目探测，并修复 macOS Core Graphics 在不同回退字体共享 PostScript 名称时偶发查表错字的问题。  #### 👥 贡献者  感谢 @idxdy（PR [#773](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/773)）、@allegrox（PR [#775](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/775)、[#793](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/793)、[#796](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/796)）、@HarpeLm（PR [#776](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/776)、[#777](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/777)）、@BilityLeu（PR [#782](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/782)、[#784](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/784)、[#790](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/790)）、@m00nLi（PR [#780](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/780)、[#781](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/781)、[#783](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/783)、[#785](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/785)、[#786](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/786)、[#799](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/799)）对本版本的贡献！  ### English  OxideTerm 2.3.3 moves Session Manager into the sidebar and redesigns Host Monitoring with a compact layout, while adding template-driven batch SSH creation, BLE plugin host APIs, Trusted Hosts management, editable multiline paste protection, terminal resize and streaming responsiveness gains, and cross-platform GPUI and IME fixes.  #### 🗂️ Session Manager, Workbench, and Navigation  - **Sidebar Session Manager**: Render Session Manager directly in the sidebar with mutual exclusion against the dedicated Session Manager tab while keeping SFTP docked below; supports local search (cleared and closed on `Esc`), whole-row drag-and-drop, Ctrl/Cmd and Shift multiselect, and context-menu batch deletion across active selections. (PR [#796](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/796)) - **Group Badge Count Fix**: Saved BLE connection profiles are now included in Session Manager group totals. (PR [#793](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/793)) - **Batch SSH Connection Creation**: Create up to 2,000 SSH connections at once from Session Manager or the CLI (`oxideterm connections batch-create`) using an OpenSSH-style `Host` template with `{host}`, `{profile}`, `{user}`, `{port}`, `{name}`, and `{group}` placeholders and one connection per line. Previews classify additions, in-place updates, skips, and invalid rows with all-or-nothing transactional writes; saved templates live in the system credential store or encrypted portable vault and strictly preserve `ProxyCommand` argument boundaries. (PR [#773](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/773)) - **Compact Host Monitoring Sidebar**: Rebuilt the Host Monitoring panel into a denser stacked view showing CPU utilization and trend sparklines, expandable per-core usage grids when supported, memory and swap capacity, uptime and system identity, disk usage and I/O rates, network RX/TX sparklines, and RTT status. - **Streamlined Active Sessions Tree**: Removed the redundant Focus View and drill-down breadcrumbs from Active Sessions in favor of unified Tree View and Host View modes, cleaner node and `Terminal #N` labels, tree guide rails, and direct inline controls. - **Welcome Workbench, Tab Scope, and Settings Polish**: Refined the Welcome page layout, added a titlebar Tab Scope selector to filter tabs with inline clear, and streamlined Settings card shells, inline rule editors, and the terminal command sender bar.  #### ⚡ Terminal, Paste, and Encoding  - **Editable Multiline Paste Preview**: When multiline paste protection is enabled, pasted drafts open ready to edit with `Enter` for newlines, `Esc` to cancel, Undo/Redo, and an explicit action to strip Markdown code fences; unmodified drafts pass through normal paste handling with original line endings intact. - **Zen Mode Top-Edge Exit Control**: Move the pointer to the top edge of the window in Zen mode to reveal an exit button without relying solely on keyboard shortcuts, while isolating the control from terminal focus and input capture. (PR [#799](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/799)) - **Resize Redraws and Streaming Responsiveness**: Fixed resized terminal grids flashing blank and prompts redrawing incompletely; moved local UI resizes off blocking parser work and cut repeated snapshot, style-hash, highlight, and streaming link-detection allocations during heavy output while bounding combining-character input. (PR [#784](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/784)) - **Legacy Encoding and Split UTF-8 Fixes**: Unrepresentable characters in legacy encodings (GBK, Big5, Shift_JIS, EUC-JP, EUC-KR, Windows-1252, and Telnet usernames) now send `?` instead of HTML numeric character entities, and valid UTF-8 sequences split across serial, Telnet, or BLE reads no longer trigger false encoding-mismatch warnings. (PR [#776](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/776), [#777](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/777))  #### 🔐 SSH, Host Keys, and Shell Integration  - **Trusted Hosts Management in Settings**: Added a **Trusted hosts** section in Connection Settings to search saved keys by host, port, or fingerprint, copy fingerprints, delete entries, or open the trust file; when a remote host key changes, the confirmation prompt offers **Replace key and connect**, replacing only the entry matching the reviewed host, port, key type, and expected fingerprint. - **ProxyJump Route Reuse and Hop Identity**: Separated jump-hop identity from route ownership so restored multihop SSH routes preserve per-hop connection settings and upstream proxy policies. - **Remote Shell Integration v7**: Gates OSC 133 command markers and private OSC adapters behind verified SSH session markers (`OXIDETERM_PRIVATE_OSC=1`) while keeping OSC 7 working-directory tracking active even if servers strip `LC_OXIDETERM_SESSION`; existing v6 scripts upgrade automatically. - **SFTP and Modal Scrolling Fixes**: Restored advanced fields and vertical scrolling in the standalone SFTP form, aligned the SSH tunnel modal backdrop with connection dialogs, and restored scrolling in multiline fields and key-import modals.  #### 🔌 Plugins, BLE, and MCP  - **BLE Plugin Host APIs, Toolbars, and Floating Overlays**: Added `ble.read` and `ble.write` capabilities alongside `ble.sessions`, `ble.describe`, `ble.read`, `ble.write`, `ble.onNotification`, and `ble.offNotification`, plus manifest-declared terminal toolbar chips and pane-bound floating overlays with automatic focus handover. GATT payloads never enter logs or error messages; because reconnect assigns a new BLE terminal session ID, plugins must re-query `ble.sessions` and re-subscribe after reconnect. (PR [#775](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/775)) - **MCP Viewport-Independent Terminal History**: Added the `terminals_history` MCP tool so AI clients can paginate through scrollback or screen contents using snapshot slots and `next_cursor` without losing their place when the user scrolls the terminal. (PR [#790](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/790))  #### 🖥️ UI Chrome, Input Methods, and Platform Compatibility  - **Dark Theme Contrast and Unified Menus**: Improved luminance separation between sidebars/titlebars and workspace surfaces on dark themes (including One Dark) with crisp collapsed activity-bar borders, and unified borders, icon rows, shortcut alignment, hover highlights, and separators across terminal, editor, IDE, SFTP, file manager, tab, Session Manager, and SSH algorithm menus. - **Toolbar Feedback and Knowledge Dialog Keyboard Handling**: Preserved per-button hover tints on filled toolbar controls and consistent hover alpha on destructive buttons; in the new knowledge-document dialog, `Enter` submits from the title field and `Tab` cycles focus strictly inside the modal until dismissed. (PR [#781](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/781), [#783](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/783), [#785](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/785), [#786](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/786)) - **Tab-Bar Trackpad Scroll and Editor IME Caret Fixes**: Tab-bar trackpad scrolling locks to the dominant axis to reduce diagonal jitter, and the built-in editor now keeps IME caret position, visibility, buffer selection isolation, and wrapped preedit anchors accurate during composition. (PR [#780](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/780), [#782](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/782)) - **GPUI 0.3.12 Upgrade and Native Platform Fixes**: Upgraded patched GPUI to recover malformed compound text in Linux XIM sessions, fix segmented font run kerning under Windows DirectWrite, handle native Windows paths in local project probing, and isolate macOS Core Graphics fallback glyph tables when distinct faces share a PostScript name.  #### 👥 Contributors  Thanks to @idxdy (PR [#773](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/773)), @allegrox (PR [#775](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/775), [#793](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/793), [#796](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/796)), @HarpeLm (PR [#776](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/776), [#777](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/777)), @BilityLeu (PR [#782](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/782), [#784](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/784), [#790](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/790)), @m00nLi (PR [#780](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/780), [#781](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/781), [#783](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/783), [#785](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/785), [#786](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/786), [#799](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/pull/799)) for contributing to this release!  ## 📥 Download for your system  | Operating system | x64 | ARM64 | |---|---|---| | **Windows** | [Setup (.exe)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.3.3/OxideTerm_2.3.3_windows_x64-setup.exe) | [Setup (.exe)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.3.3/OxideTerm_2.3.3_windows_arm64-setup.exe) | | **macOS** | [DMG (Intel)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.3.3/OxideTerm_2.3.3_macos_x64.dmg) | [DMG (Apple Silicon)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.3.3/OxideTerm_2.3.3_macos_arm64.dmg) | | **Linux** | [AppImage](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.3.3/OxideTerm_2.3.3_linux_x64.AppImage) · [DEB](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.3.3/OxideTerm_2.3.3_linux_x64.deb) · [RPM](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.3.3/OxideTerm_2.3.3_linux_x64.rpm) | [AppImage](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.3.3/OxideTerm_2.3.3_linux_arm64.AppImage) · [DEB](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.3.3/OxideTerm_2.3.3_linux_arm64.deb) · [RPM](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.3.3/OxideTerm_2.3.3_linux_arm64.rpm) |  Portable archives, signatures, and `sha256sums.txt` remain available in the release assets below.  <details> <summary>📌 Installation Tips / 安装提示</summary>  ### macOS  Downloaded `.dmg` files may be quarantined by Gatekeeper. Run in Terminal:  bash xattr -cr ~/Downloads/OxideTerm_*.dmg # or after install / 或安装后 xattr -cr /Applications/OxideTerm.app   ### Windows  If SmartScreen warns, click **More info** -> **Run anyway**.  若 SmartScreen 弹出警告，点击 **更多信息** -> **仍要运行**。  ### Linux  bash # AppImage chmod +x OxideTerm_*_linux_*.AppImage && ./OxideTerm_*_linux_*.AppImage  # Debian/Ubuntu sudo dpkg -i OxideTerm_*_linux_*.deb && sudo apt-get install -f  # Fedora/RHEL-compatible systems sudo dnf install ./OxideTerm_*_linux_*.rpm   </details>  ## 🔗 Links  - Documentation: https://oxideterm.app - GitHub Issues: https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues - Changelog: https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/blob/main/.github/release-notes/stable-changelog.md
```

### 2026-11-15T20:07:05Z [**t8y2/dbx**](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx)

```
### 新功能  - 设置新增工具栏自定义，可配置新建连接、新建查询、备份等按钮的显隐与顺序 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14667](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14667)) - 全局快速打开升级为统一搜索入口，可在连接、数据库、表、字段、SQL 文件与插件命令间结构化搜索，支持拼音匹配和范围筛选 (contributed by @eryajf) (PR [#14826](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14826)) - 顶部菜单新增可搜索的最近关闭标签选择器，关闭全部标签后也能快速找回 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14558](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14558)) - 侧栏连接和数据库节点新增新建数据库一级入口，并支持 Shift+F4 快捷键 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14913](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14913)) - 侧栏选中数据库或表分组时可用快捷键直接打开新建表编辑器 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14910](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14910)) - 侧栏列名双击行为可配置为生成条件查询语句 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14552](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14552)) - 执行 SQL 文件对话框新增目标 Schema 选择，批量导入可落入指定 Schema (contributed by @xiaou61) (PR [#14590](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14590)) - 数据网格新增条件面板 WHERE/ORDER BY 宽度比例设置和 ORDER BY 显隐开关 (contributed by @zhongtait) (PR [#14612](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14612)) - 数据库导出支持限制每张表的导出行数，并可按指定字段取最早或最新的 N 行 (contributed by @dienaso) (PR [#14799](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14799)) - SQL 编辑器支持自定义上一条/下一条补全项的快捷键 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14763](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14763)) - 字段补全新增设置：无歧义字段可不加表名前缀 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14685](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14685)) - SQL 编辑器双击括号可选中括号内的内容 (contributed by @x-labb) (PR [#14783](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14783)) - Oracle 表右键新增表授权查看，按受权者展示表级与列级权限 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14953](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14953)) - Oracle 存储过程、函数、序列、包和同义词支持导出对象 DDL 为 SQL 文件 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14958](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14958)) - Oracle 对象浏览器支持从右键菜单直接编译无效的存储过程、函数和视图 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14954](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14954)) - PostgreSQL 侧栏新增行级安全策略查看入口 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14935](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14935)) - PostgreSQL 模式下新增用户自定义聚合函数列表入口 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14949](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14949)) - PostgreSQL 序列查看显示步长、范围、缓存等选项，并提供新建序列模板 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14941](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14941)) - Redis 键列表支持按类型筛选，Stream 条目同时显示对应的本地时间 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14942](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14942))  ### 改进  - MySQL 表列表新增 AUTO_INCREMENT 值列 (contributed by @x-labb) (PR [#14850](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14850)) - MySQL 补全支持 IGNORE INDEX 和 USE INDEX 索引提示 (contributed by @x-labb) (PR [#14633](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14633)) - MySQL WHERE 条件开头输入字符串字段时提供字段 = '' 补全 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14947](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14947)) - MySQL INSERT IGNORE 列清单中可补全表字段 (contributed by @jeff377) (PR [#14655](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14655)) - PostgreSQL 未选 Schema 时表补全可跨 Schema 查找，Schema 选择器支持清除 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14934](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14934)) - PostgreSQL 表 DDL 元数据批量查询失败时标明具体失败项，不再只显示 db error (contributed by @hongshuo-wang) (PR [#14660](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14660)) - Oracle INSERT 语句的 VALUES 与 SYSDATE 等表达式补全更准确 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14661](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14661)) - Oracle 短 RAW 值（如 SYS_GUID() 结果）在网格中直接显示十六进制 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14761](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14761)) - Oracle 对象列表元数据查询按请求的对象类型裁剪，大 Schema 下查询负担更低 (contributed by @Acd-kk) (PR [#14979](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14979)) - 达梦字段补全按未加引号标识符规则处理大小写，小写用户名也能匹配字段 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14937](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14937)) - 达梦编辑器高亮补齐原生数据类型、自增关键字和日期函数 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14938](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14938)) - SQL Server 批量执行遇旧版驱动时提示升级驱动，不再报反序列化错误 (contributed by @jeff377) (PR [#14621](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14621)) - SQL Server 结构比较延迟加载两侧同名表的 DDL，元数据查询数量减半 (contributed by @BrydonJ) (PR [#14408](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14408)) - Kafka 主题页新增按连接保存的自动加载主题开关 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14936](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14936)) - RabbitMQ 订阅列表新增消费者客户端地址列 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14548](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14548)) - StarRocks 视图 DDL 显示时对查询部分做格式化换行 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14659](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14659)) - TiDB 对象浏览器的行数列明确标注为估算行数 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14917](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14917)) - MongoDB 只读文档预览按 Mongo Shell 风格显示 ObjectId、BSON 日期等类型 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14920](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14920)) - CTE 体内的列补全仅引用当前 CTE 体及其之前声明的数据源 (contributed by @x-labb) (PR [#14632](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14632)) - CSV 导入支持多字符分隔符（如 |@|） (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14273](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14273)) - ClickHouse 连接测试错误展示完整传输层原因链（DNS/TCP/TLS 等） - AI 预设列表更新：移除 Atlas Cloud 集成 - AI 预设列表更新：下线 Jalapeno Cloud，优先展示 AICodeMirror - AI 助手识别虚谷（XuguDB）方言的 SQL 转换请求 (contributed by @mapan0424) (PR [#14699](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14699)) - 更新中心显示真实下载进度和更新全部的批量进度 (contributed by @hongshuo-wang) (PR [#14570](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14570)) - 更新中心弹窗高度随内容自适应，正文滚动时标题和操作按钮保持可见 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14765](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14765)) - SQL 参数对话框新增一键清除参数值历史 (contributed by @xiaou61) (PR [#14903](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14903)) - 结构比较结果与部署确认步骤顶部显示源/目标两端连接信息 (contributed by @xiaou61) (PR [#14886](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14886)) - SQL 编辑器数据库选择器选项可直接复制数据库名 (contributed by @heeeex100) (PR [#14387](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14387)) - 新查询保存到 SQL 库时默认归入同名数据库文件夹，图标按连接颜色区分 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14956](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14956)) - 表详情面板新增新建查询入口 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14906](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14906)) - 表数据页隐藏列后复制查询 SQL 只包含可见列 (contributed by @x-labb) (PR [#14940](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14940)) - 数据网格检索浮层可拖动到表格内其他位置 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14904](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14904)) - WHERE 字段下拉按网格自定义列顺序排列 (contributed by @xiaou61) (PR [#14887](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14887)) - 快捷筛选与排序构建器的候选字段跟随表格列布局排序 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14914](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14914)) - 表头排序图标左键点击直接切换升/降序，右键仍可打开完整菜单 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14680](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14680)) - 结果集筛选可视化：已筛选列头显示漏斗标识，可单独关闭某个字段的筛选 (contributed by @dienaso) (PR [#14975](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14975)) - 表格拖动表头调整列顺序后先确认再保存，避免误拖改变布局 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14978](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14978)) - 对象列表列设置按钮固定在表头右侧，不再遮挡列标题 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14813](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14813)) - 对象列表常用列（行数、注释等）前置，并补显示创建和更新时间 (contributed by @Acd-kk) (PR [#14959](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14959)) - 对象列表新增重置顺序操作，一键恢复内置列顺序 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14694](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14694)) - Oracle 对象浏览器默认视图搜索时匹配已加载的各类对象，不再只搜表名 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14932](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14932)) - 快速打开支持按 MySQL 字段名、字段注释和表注释搜索 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14951](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14951)) - 多语句执行摘要的耗时列按量级自适应显示单位 (contributed by @MomentarySixthSense) (PR [#14902](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14902)) - 新建查询等入口统一图标并显示快捷键提示 (contributed by @eryajf) (PR [#14990](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14990)) - 桌面主窗口最小宽度降至 760 逻辑像素，适配更窄窗口 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14948](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14948)) - 侧栏拖拽把手在浅色背景下清晰可见 (contributed by @sx4im) (PR [#14928](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14928)) - 拖动结果表横向滚动条时不再弹出 SQL 悬浮预览遮挡内容 (contributed by @chukui2005) (PR [#14640](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14640)) - Nacos 配置页窄分栏下筛选输入框显示更完整 (contributed by @chukui2005) (PR [#14855](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14855)) - SQL 编辑器主题样式跨标签生命周期复用，恢复缓存标签不再累积重复样式 (contributed by @Acd-kk) (PR [#14463](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14463)) - 西班牙语界面补齐 224 个缺失词条，回到与英文对齐 (contributed by @0scar07) (PR [#14214](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14214))  ### 修复  - MySQL 服务器默认 autocommit=0 时，自动提交标签页的写入不再被静默回滚 (contributed by @Acd-kk) (PR [#14662](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14662)) - MySQL 查询标签切换数据库后保留会话变量、临时表和事务状态 (contributed by @Acd-kk) (PR [#14927](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14927)) - MySQL 大表导出中断后立即停止服务端查询，不再因排空结果集超时卡住导出队列 (contributed by @jeff377) (PR [#14861](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14861)) - MySQL 诊断不再把 TIMESTAMPDIFF/TIMESTAMPADD 的时间单位误报为未知字段 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14810](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14810)) - PostgreSQL 删除外表生成 DROP FOREIGN TABLE，侧栏删除外表不再报错 (contributed by @xiaou61) (PR [#14771](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14771)) - CockroachDB 索引与列元数据查询改用 concat()，不再报 unknown function: textcat (contributed by @Pius-x) (PR [#14875](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14875)) - GaussDB 列查询改用 ::int 简写，侧栏可正常列出表字段 (contributed by @xiaou61) (PR [#15000](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/15000)) - PostgreSQL 视图与列注释在单对象 DDL 和数据库导出中完整保留 (contributed by @CN-Scars) (PR [#14753](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14753)) - openGauss 普通账号备份按 USAGE 权限过滤 Schema，不再因无权 Schema 整体失败 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#14275](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14275)) - Greenplum、Cloudberry、Hologres 停用元组定位符，无主键表编辑不再报错 - 数据网格排序被数据库拒绝时自动去掉行定位符重试 (contributed by @sx4im) (PR [#14939](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14939)) - Kingbase 查询工具栏不再误清除已保存的默认数据库 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14816](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14816)) - DROP INDEX CONCURRENTLY 语句不再被编辑器误标为语法错误 (contributed by @dienaso) (PR [#14856](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14856)) - Oracle 会话遵循客户端日期语言，中文 NLS 日期字面量不再报 ORA-01843 (contributed by @Lan-zk) (PR [#14863](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14863)) - Oracle 语义诊断解析 PUBLIC 同义词，DUAL 等不再误报未知表 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#14864](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14864)) - Oracle 对象浏览器恢复显示表、视图和物化视图的注释 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14687](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14687)) - Oracle 以多层括号或前置注释开头的查询可正常返回结果行 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14436](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14436)) - Oracle 与 SQL Server 同义词的 DDL 和列信息解析到背后的底层对象 (contributed by @jeff377) (PR [#14896](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14896)) - Oracle 11g 下 PL/SQL 调试辅助标识符缩短到 30 字符内，调试功能恢复可用 - SQL Server 临时表补全不再因 tempdb 目录锁阻塞整个连接的元数据请求 (contributed by @jeff377) (PR [#14466](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14466)) - 达梦实例缺少 ALL_OBJECTS 字典时回退 JDBC 元数据，表列表可正常显示 (contributed by @xiaou61) (PR [#14998](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14998)) - 达梦 CURRENT_USER 被拒绝时回退 USER，作业页面可正常打开 - 达梦 dexp/dimp 对含 @ 或 / 的密码加引号处理，导出导入不再连接失败 - ClickHouse 执行 USE 后查询跟随切换到新数据库，不再返回旧库数据 - InfluxDB 语句错误随 HTTP 200 返回时如实报错，不再显示成功 0 行 (contributed by @xiaou61) (PR [#15001](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/15001)) - Teradata 表预览与有界读改用 TOP/ROW_NUMBER 分页，不再生成报 3706 错误的 LIMIT (contributed by @dienaso) (PR [#14630](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14630)) - Doris 与 StarRocks 表在侧栏大小模式下正常显示表大小 (contributed by @xiaou61) (PR [#14965](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14965)) - Redis 值面板窄分栏下自动换行开关不再竖排换行并遮挡下方控件 (contributed by @Abeautifulsnow) (PR [#14760](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14760)) - MongoDB 集合筛选自动识别 BSON 日期类型，日期范围条件按日期比较 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14909](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14909)) - Nacos 服务实例筛选改为过滤已加载实例，未知集群不再残留旧列表 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14907](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14907)) - Consul 配置 SSH 传输层后 HTTP 请求正确经传输层路由，不再直连失败 (contributed by @eryajf) (PR [#14403](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14403)) - Navicat 兼容 HTTP 隧道接受 2.04 以外的脚本版本 (contributed by @heoo0124) (PR [#14897](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14897)) - 手动指定的 JDBC 驱动依赖与插件 classpath 隔离，不再触发 NoSuchMethodError (contributed by @CN-Scars) (PR [#14772](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14772)) - MCP 对 MySQL 静态 CTE 查询的数据库白名单校验不再误报越库 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14916](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14916)) - MCP 高风险 SQL 待审批时桌面端请求系统注意，主窗口在后台也能看到确认框 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14943](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14943)) - 分离 SQL 窗口中 AI 功能恢复可用，并在窗口标题栏提供 AI 开关 (contributed by @Acd-kk) (PR [#14873](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14873)) - 并发创建复用同一实际连接池时不再误报连接池发布凭据错误 (contributed by @lcq928) (PR [#14546](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14546)) - 保存连接配置不再拆除在线插件连接池导致已连接终端被重置 (contributed by @jinpy666) (PR [#14827](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14827)) - 从快捷连接新建查询时正确应用连接配置的默认 Schema (contributed by @lxk955) (PR [#11865](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/11865)) - 代码片段同步的加密敏感信息勾选偏好保存后重新打开不再丢失 (contributed by @jingouzhui) (PR [#14030](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14030)) - 应用崩溃或重启后恢复后台导出任务及其日志 (contributed by @xiaou61) (PR [#14997](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14997)) - 手动事务空闲过期后导出给出明确提示并清理失效会话 (contributed by @Lan-zk) (PR [#14882](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14882)) - 导入多表 SQL 转储文件自动转交 SQL 文件执行，不再卡在单表向导报错 (contributed by @Acd-kk) (PR [#14397](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14397)) - 加载全部行完成后页脚统计状态正常结束，不再一直转圈 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14930](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14930)) - 仅修改外层 WHERE 条件重复执行时保留自定义列布局 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14931](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14931)) - 条件批量编辑空 WHERE 条件时提示填写，不再按整表计算影响行数 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14950](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14950)) - 导出菜单为选区、选中列等每个导出范围单独成列显示 (contributed by @xiaou61) (PR [#14470](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14470)) - 多库执行搜索筛选后全选仅选中可见目标，已选列表底部不再被裁切 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14669](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14669)) - 粘贴带尾部空行的 SQL 后当前语句快捷键仍能定位并执行语句 (contributed by @heeeex100) (PR [#14784](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14784)) - 文件内容搜索预览恢复选区高亮并支持 Ctrl+C 复制 (contributed by @wushuang3625) (PR [#14817](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14817)) - 超大 SQL 标签内容改存外部文件，不再导致每次启动解析卡死 (contributed by @xiaou61) (PR [#14918](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14918)) - 设置搜索框不再触发浏览器历史与凭据自动填充 (contributed by @sx4im) (PR [#14952](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14952)) - 手动安装稳定版后正确识别已晋升为稳定的 beta 版本号 (contributed by @sx4im) (PR [#14929](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14929)) - Windows 更新缓存提交遇杀软短暂锁定自动重试，应用内更新不再失败 (contributed by @knqiufan) (PR [#14821](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14821)) - 结构查看浮层与模态对话框不再争抢层级导致面板无法操作 (contributed by @xiaou61) (PR [#14967](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14967)) - 侧栏自定义刷新快捷键正确生效，刷新表节点不再强制展开 (contributed by @MomentarySixthSense) (PR [#14924](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14924)) - 全局侧栏搜索命中的未加载表可正常展开列和索引分组 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14269](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14269)) - 表按新列顺序重建后清除旧的本地列顺序，不再叠加错乱 (contributed by @jeff377) (PR [#14866](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14866)) - 表结构编辑器空草稿恢复不再白屏，元数据读取错误保留展示 (contributed by @Acd-kk) (PR [#14858](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14858)) - 结构比较中删表操作默认不勾选，需手动选择才会生成 DROP TABLE (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14756](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14756)) - Transwarp Inceptor 方言正确注册，连接可正常解析方言能力 (contributed by @ThugKd) (PR [#14981](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14981)) - 插件能力为空时编码为空数组，插件初始化不再失败 (contributed by @sx4im) (PR [#14973](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/14973))
```

### 2026-11-17T20:22:47Z [**razvandimescu/numa**](https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa)

```
This release fixes the x86_64 Linux binary of 0.25.1, which crashed with "Illegal instruction" on CPUs without AVX2. Forward mode also stops waiting on a slow upstream and learns which primary answers first, blocklists are fetched through the configured upstream, and the dashboard shows each upstream's answers and median time.  ## ⚠️ Check before upgrading  **A slow upstream no longer holds the query.** After 200 ms without an answer, Numa also asks the next upstream and uses the first answer. On 2,000 names with Quad9 first and Cloudflare second, the p99 went from 869 to 476 ms for 7% more upstream queries. `hedge_ms = 0` under `[upstream]` keeps strict list order. A single upstream gets no second query, so providers with a query quota see no extra queries. (#505)  **The primary that answers first is asked first.** Upstreams of the same kind as the first one (private address, plain, or encrypted) race: about 1 query in 20, at most one a minute, goes to two of them at once. One that answers first 7 times before it loses twice is asked first. For a fixed order, put the others in `fallback`. Thanks @bcookatpcsd (#493, #506)  ## 🐛 Fixes  - The x86_64 Linux binary runs on CPUs without AVX2 again. 0.25.1 crashed on start, also in the amd64 Docker image and Homebrew on Linux. Each Linux binary now has to start on a baseline CPU in qemu (x86-64 v1, Cortex-A53, ARM1176) before it is published. Thanks @id4vip (#518, #519, #520) - Blocklist hosts resolve through the configured upstream and cache. Before, they went to the bootstrap servers in clear text even with a DoH, DoT or ODoH upstream, and never refreshed on a network that blocks port 53. Thanks @Guara92 (#507, #509) - On Windows, a reply that fails with os error 10022 after a VPN changes routes is sent again, so the client gets its answer. Thanks @HighPriest (#501, #508) - The API answers 400 to an override or a blocking pause with a duration the clock can't represent. (#511)  ## 📊 Dashboard  - In forward mode the Outbound Wire panel shows each upstream with its share of the answers and its median time, and `/stats` has an `upstreams` list. Before, every forwarded answer counted against the upstream asked first. Thanks @bcookatpcsd (#289, #517)  ## Also notable  - Records and DNSSEC canonical data are written by one encoder. In benchmarks, building signed data takes about 45% less time and DS checks 20% less. (#513, #514) - The release workflow pins its actions to commit SHAs. (#510)  <details> <summary>All changes</summary>  ## What's Changed * perf(forward): Ask the next upstream after 200 ms without an answer by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/505 * ci(release): Pin the release actions to commit SHAs by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/510 * fix(api): reject durations the clock can't represent by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/511 * refactor: reuse is_paused() and saturating_duration_since by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/512 * fix(udp): resend unpinned then pinned when the reply source is rejected by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/508 * refactor(record): one header and RDLENGTH path for every record type by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/513 * refactor(dnssec): encode canonical RDATA with DnsRecord's writer by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/514 * refactor(system_dns): share the process spawn helpers across platforms by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/515 * fix(blocklist): resolve list hosts through the configured upstream by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/509 * feat(dashboard): show the answers and median time of each upstream by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/517 * perf(forward): Ask first the primary that answers first by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/506 * fix(release): build x86_64 Linux binary for baseline CPUs by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/519 * ci(release): smoke-test aarch64 and armv6 on their oldest CPUs by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/520   **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/compare/v0.25.1...v0.25.2   </details> 
```

### 2026-11-12T08:58:01Z [**jlcodes99/vscode-antigravity-cockpit**](https://github.com/jlcodes99/vscode-antigravity-cockpit)

```
## 中文  ### 新增 - **配额耗尽预测**：历史图表为各配额序列显示预计消耗速率和耗尽时间，支持官方周配额与 5 小时窗口。预测仅使用足够的近期样本，配额重置后停止沿用旧周期，图表投影按可见时间范围显示。 - **状态栏 Credits 开关**：通过 `agCockpit.showPromptCredits` 控制状态栏及其悬浮提示是否显示 Credits。 - **Gemini 4 Argon (Weekly)**：单独显示官方 Argon 周配额，模型清单缺少 Argon 时也能显示。本地监控可使用同账号尚未重置的官方配额缓存。  ### 调整 - **Model Quota 展示跟随官方分组**：Dashboard、QuickPick、状态栏、账号树、账号总览和配额历史在数据可用时使用官方配额组与窗口。固定分组卡片保留重命名、拖拽排序和状态栏置顶交互。 - **默认使用本地监控**：保留用户已有的配额来源选择。本地模型变体的剩余配额和重置时间相同时合并为一行展示。 - **打包与发布质量检查**：CI 执行扩展和 Webview 类型检查、Lint、测试及生产构建；本地打包也会在构建前检查类型与 Lint。  ### 修复 - **周配额与 5 小时窗口**：授权刷新获取官方配额摘要并写入缓存；摘要缺失时可使用同账号桌面端缓存中的摘要补齐。 - **配额窗口与账号历史**：已知周配额和 5 小时窗口按官方 ID 识别，缺少余额的配额桶不再显示为 0%。历史记录按实际提供配额的账号归档。WSL 从 Windows 用户目录读取桌面端配额缓存。 - **新模型识别**：官方排序表遗漏的模型仍可展示；新发现的模型自动加入自定义可见列表，云端内部、禁用和黑名单模型继续隐藏。 - **新版本地进程支持**：兼容旧语言服务进程与新版 agy hub，进程重启后可重新连接。仅在本次本地请求成功且账号身份匹配时，用本地独有模型补充云端结果。 - **配额来源设置**：本地与授权监控选择在修改设置和重启后均生效。 - **按账号展示配额和积分**：授权模式下，状态栏和积分缓存使用选中的监控账号。切换至 IDE 当前登录账号时刷新配额，授权过期时保留其他已保存账号。Google One AI 积分条目省略金额时按零处理，未获取到的余额显示为未知。 - **Remote SSH 打开 Cockpit Tools**：通过本机注册的链接打开桌面应用。 - **README 星标历史与公告链接**：更新星标历史图表服务和公告中的作者商店链接。  ### 安全 - **减少账号同步中的 Token 读取**：先使用不含 Token 的账号列表，仅在需要导入远端账号时请求明文 Token。 - **缓存和设置私密写入**：文件采用原子写入，在 POSIX 系统使用仅属主可访问的权限。WebSocket 入站指令拒绝畸形 payload；连接鉴权仍需桌面端配合实现。  ### 移除 - **旧自定义/自动分组成员管理界面**：Dashboard 与 QuickPick 不再手动重映射模型成员或生成自定义分组，当前分组结构由官方配额组提供。   ## English  ### Added - **Quota exhaustion forecasts**: History charts show estimated consumption rates and exhaustion times for each quota series, including official weekly and five-hour windows. Forecasts require sufficient recent samples, stop after a quota reset, and extend the chart only to the visible forecast boundary. - **Status bar Credits setting**: `agCockpit.showPromptCredits` controls whether Credits appear in the status bar and its tooltip. - **Gemini 4 Argon (Weekly)**: Shows the official Argon weekly quota separately from the regular Gemini quota, even when the model list omits Argon. Local monitoring can use the same account’s cached official bucket before it resets.  ### Changed - **Model Quota display follows official groups**: Dashboard, QuickPick, Status Bar, Account Tree, Accounts Overview, and quota history use official quota groups and windows when available. Fixed group cards retain rename, drag-to-reorder, and status-bar pin interactions. - **Local monitoring is the default**: Existing quota source selections are preserved. Local model variants with matching balances and reset times share one display row. - **Quality checks before packaging and release**: Extension and Webview type checks, lint, tests, and production builds run in CI; local packaging also checks types and lint before building.  ### Fixed - **Weekly and five-hour quota windows**: Authorized refresh retrieves the official quota summary and preserves it in the cache. When missing, a same-account desktop cache can supply the summary. - **Quota windows and account history**: Known weekly and five-hour buckets use their official IDs, and buckets with missing balances no longer show 0%. History follows the account that supplied the quota. WSL reads desktop quota caches from the Windows user profile. - **New model discovery**: Models omitted from the official ordering list remain visible. Newly discovered models join customized visible lists while internal, disabled, and blacklisted cloud models remain hidden. - **New local process support**: Detects both legacy language servers and the newer agy hub process, with reconnection after process restarts. Local-only models supplement cloud results only after a successful local request with a matching account identity. - **Quota source preferences**: Local and authorized monitoring choices are respected across settings changes and restarts. - **Account-specific quota and Credits**: Authorized status bar updates and cached Credits use the selected monitoring account. Switching to the current IDE login refreshes quota, and expired authorization keeps other saved accounts intact. Omitted amounts on Google One AI credit entries are treated as zero, while unavailable balances remain unknown. - **Cockpit Tools over Remote SSH**: The extension opens the desktop app through its registered link on the local computer. - **README star history and announcement links**: Updated the star history chart provider and the author's shop link in the announcement.  ### Security - **Reduced token reads during account sync**: Token-free account lists are used first; plaintext tokens are requested only when remote accounts need to be imported. - **Private cache and settings writes**: Files are written atomically with owner-only permissions on POSIX systems. Inbound WebSocket commands reject malformed payloads; desktop-side connection authentication remains required.  ### Removed - **Legacy custom/auto grouping membership UI**: Dashboard and QuickPick no longer remap model membership manually or generate custom groups; official quota groups supply the active grouping structure. 
```

```
<div align="center">   <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tw93/Kaku/main/assets/logo.png" alt="Kaku Logo" width="120" height="120" />   <h1 style="margin: 12px 0 6px;">Kaku V0.30.0</h1>   <p><em>A fast, out-of-the-box terminal built for AI coding.</em></p> </div>  ### Changelog 1. **Full Screen Freeze**: After an update notice in full screen, Kaku keeps responding to keys, scrolling and tab switches, and showing the window from a Lua action no longer crashes. 2. **Chinese and Emoji Backspace**: In programs that read whole lines, such as `read` and `cat`, Backspace removes a whole Chinese character or emoji instead of leaving broken bytes. 3. **Quieter Agent Title**: When Claude Code already shows the session topic on its input box border, the window's top right and the remote page keep only the agent mark. 4. **Unbound Cmd Keys**: A Cmd shortcut with no binding no longer types its letter into the program, such as a stray j in the Claude Code prompt. 5. **Updating While Open**: When `brew upgrade` replaces the app under a running Kaku, reloading the config keeps every default setting instead of switching to a cream tab bar. 6. **Password Prompts**: The cursor stays as it is while you type a password, and `config.detect_password_input = true` brings back the lock. 7. **Tab Fixes**: Hovering the left edge of a retro tab highlights that tab, and switching on the Mac to a tab a browser had resized fills the window again.  ### 更新日志 1. **全屏卡死**：全屏时点开更新提示后，按键、滚动和切换标签都能正常响应，通过 Lua 动作显示窗口也不会再崩溃。 2. **中文与表情删除**：在 `read`、`cat` 这类按行读取输入的程序里，Backspace 会删掉整个中文字符或表情，不再留下残缺字节。 3. **Agent 标题去重**：Claude Code 的输入框边框已经显示会话话题时，窗口右上角和远程页面只保留 Agent 图标。 4. **未绑定的 Cmd 快捷键**：按下没有绑定的 Cmd 组合键不会再把字母打进程序，比如 Claude Code 输入框里多出的 j。 5. **运行中升级**：Kaku 开着时被 `brew upgrade` 更新，重载配置后默认设置都会保留，标签栏不再变成米色样式。 6. **密码输入**：输入密码时光标保持原样，需要锁形光标可以设置 `config.detect_password_input = true`。 7. **标签修复**：鼠标悬停在复古标签左侧时高亮的就是这个标签，在 Mac 上切到被浏览器调整过尺寸的标签也会重新铺满窗口。  Special thanks to @HiAriesZhou for the contributions to this release.  > https://github.com/tw93/Kaku
```

```
Obsidian Skills now has a skill for Obsidian Headless, so agents can sync and publish vaults without the desktop app. The plugin also installs in Gemini CLI.  If you installed from the Claude Code marketplace, run `claude plugin update obsidian@obsidian-skills`.  ### Plugin  - Added a Gemini CLI extension: `gemini extensions install https://github.com/obsidianmd/obsidian-skills`.  ### Skills  - Headless: added a skill for `ob`, the Obsidian Headless CLI, to sync and publish vaults from the command line. It asks the user to type passwords and runs `publish` with `--dry-run` first (#75, #155). - Bases: added kanban views and `groupOrder` (#153), and documented the `note.` prefix that `displayName` needs in `properties` (#84). - Obsidian CLI: on WSL, call `Obsidian.com` instead of `Obsidian.exe` so output isn't lost (#96).  Thanks @catlee, @bencharoenwong, @hst368 and @citizen204! 
```

### 2026-11-14T23:15:54Z [**GitMetaio/Surfing**](https://github.com/GitMetaio/Surfing)

```
# What's Changed ### 🌐 New: Block by App (UID) - Adds `box.block` and a list file `block.list`: traffic is rejected by UID on the `filter OUTPUT` chain — TCP gets an RST, everything else gets ICMP / ICMPv6 unreachable - The list is maintained by the app under **App Filter → UID App Blocking**; changes take effect immediately after saving, no module restart required - `com.mobiletools.systemhelper` is a mandatory default entry, locked in the app - Known limitation: hotspot forwarded traffic (no UID on the forwarding path) and root traffic are not covered  ### 📞 New: Wi-Fi Calling support (IKE/NAT-T bypass) - Adds an IKE/NAT-T bypass, fixing VoWiFi registration failures when TPROXY is enabled - `bypass_ike_natt` is off by default; ports are configurable via `bypass_ike_natt_ports` (default `500 4500`)  ### ⚠️ Breaking Changes - Clash / mihomo only: the sing-box / v2ray / xray / hysteria branches have been removed - Magisk Lite is no longer supported: installation aborts when Lite is detected - Boot scripts now only run from the boot flow; manual execution is refused  ### 🛠️ Install & Upgrade - The bundled SurfingTile is now installed by comparing version codes: skipped when the installed app is not older; if not installed, asks via volume keys (10s timeout) - Fixed where the clash config is restored during installation, avoiding config rollback after upgrades - Ships a bundled curl binary, used by the network monitor to clear connections  ### 🐛 Fixes - iptables connmark save/restore now only touches its own mark bits, no longer clobbering marks written by other modules; this also fixes MMS messages failing to arrive - Hotspot compatibility: `ap_list` gains `swlan+` / `softap+`, with automatic migration of existing configs - Wi-Fi bypass self-healing: recreates missing bypass rules, respects the user's disable marker instead of force-starting the service, and refreshes Wi-Fi state when the service starts or stops  ### 📱 Bundled App - SurfingTile v7.2.1 ([View detailed changelog](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/GitMetaio/Surfing/refs/heads/main/app/version/com.github.surfing/release.json)) 
```
