### 2026-11-13T08:57:18Z [**Huanshere/VideoLingo**](https://github.com/Huanshere/VideoLingo)

```
# VideoLingo v3.2.0 🎨  Subtitles that look right in every language, an optional PyTorch-free engine for local Qwen3-ASR, and an installer that leaves your global pip settings alone.  ### 🚀 What's new  - **Built-in multilingual subtitle typesetting.** Burned-in subtitles no longer use one system font for everything. Each of 33 languages has its own font, size and spacing, whether it is the translated row or the source row. Lines are broken by their rendered width, measured with HarfBuzz, preferably at punctuation and between words; Chinese, Japanese and Thai are split into words with BudouX. Arabic, Devanagari, Thai and other complex scripts are shaped correctly whatever fonts your system has. - **The fonts come with VideoLingo.** 4.4 MB of OFL fonts ship in `fonts/`. The Chinese and Japanese faces are too large for the repository: they are downloaded once, on first use, and checked by SHA-256, with jsDelivr mirrors as a fallback. Docker images fetch them at build time. - **Subtitle length is now a width.** `subtitle.max_length` is measured as the line is drawn: a Latin letter counts about 1, and 80 is one full landscape row (about 35 Chinese characters on the source row). The new defaults are `max_length: 80` and `target_multiplier: 1.0`. - **The classic filter still works, and no longer runs off the screen.** Set a `font_name` to use your own font with FFmpeg's `subtitles` filter, as before. The same filter is the fallback when the typesetter can't be used, and it now gets long Chinese, Japanese and Thai lines pre-broken so they stay inside the frame. - **Optional audio.cpp engine for local Qwen3-ASR.** With `whisper.qwen_engine: audiocpp`, recognition and forced alignment go to an audio.cpp server you run yourself (GGUF weights, no PyTorch); its address is `whisper.audiocpp_url`. It is opt-in: `auto` never picks it. - **The installer keeps your pip configuration.** `--auto-mirror` used to run `pip config set global.index-url`, which changed the package index for every Python project on the machine. The chosen mirror is now used for this install only. - **See where your models are.** `installer.py --check` and a finished install list the Hugging Face cache in use with the models already in it, the local model folder, and where FFmpeg comes from. The start guide explains how to reuse models you already have, including those of a standalone WhisperX. - Fixed "Translation not found" warnings for TTS provider names in the sidebar.  ### ⚠️ Before upgrading  - **Install the new dependencies** `uharfbuzz`, `budoux` and `fonttools`: run the installer again, or `pip install -r requirements.txt`. Until then, subtitles are burned the old way and a warning asks you to re-run the installer. - **The first Chinese or Japanese burn needs a network connection** to download that font (10–20 MB, once). Offline, put the variable font from google/fonts or fonts.google.com in `fonts/`, for example `NotoSansSC[wght].ttf` or `NotoSansJP-VariableFont_wght.ttf`. - **If your `config.yaml` still has `max_length: 75` and `target_multiplier: 1.2`**, click **Restore defaults (20/80)** under *Subtitle length tuning* to use the new values. - `font_size` now scales the typesetter's own sizes (15 and 17 are its defaults), and the typesetter does not use `margin_v`. Setting a `font_name` turns the typesetter off for every language. - If an earlier `--auto-mirror` install changed your global pip index, `pip config list` shows it and `pip config unset global.index-url` removes it.  ### ✅ Release validation  - Automated suite: **832 passed, 20 skipped** (macOS arm64, Python 3.12, the FFmpeg installed by the installer). - Subtitles were burned with FFmpeg and libass and compared before and after for Chinese, Japanese, Korean, Russian, Arabic, Hindi and Thai, for the typesetter and for the fallback filter.  ---  # VideoLingo v3.2.0 🎨  每种语言的字幕都排得好看；本地 Qwen3-ASR 新增可选的免 PyTorch 推理引擎；安装器不再改动全局 pip 设置。  ### 🚀 这次更新  - **内置多语言字幕排版。** 烧录字幕不再所有语言共用一种系统字体。33 种语言无论作译文行还是原文行，都有各自的字体、字号和字距。切行按实际渲染宽度计算（HarfBuzz 测宽），优先在标点和词间断开，中文、日文、泰文用 BudouX 分词。阿拉伯文、天城文、泰文等复杂文字，不管系统装了什么字体都能正确显示。 - **字体随 VideoLingo 提供。** `fonts/` 里自带 4.4 MB 的 OFL 授权字体。中文和日文字体太大，不放进仓库，第一次用到时下载一次，用 SHA-256 校验，GitHub 下载失败时改走 jsDelivr 镜像。Docker 镜像在构建时就会下载好。 - **字幕长度改为按宽度计算。** `subtitle.max_length` 按实际画出来的宽度算：一个拉丁字母约为 1，80 是横屏的一整行（原文行约 35 个汉字）。新的默认值是 `max_length: 80`、`target_multiplier: 1.0`。 - **原来的滤镜照样能用，长行也不再溢出。** 填写 `font_name` 就和以前一样，用 FFmpeg 的 `subtitles` 滤镜和你指定的字体。排版器用不了时也会退回这个滤镜；中文、日文、泰文的长行现在会先折好，不会再超出画面。 - **本地 Qwen3-ASR 新增可选的 audio.cpp 引擎。** 设置 `whisper.qwen_engine: audiocpp` 后，识别和强制对齐都交给你自己运行的 audio.cpp 服务（GGUF 权重，不需要 PyTorch），地址在 `whisper.audiocpp_url` 里设置。需要手动开启，`auto` 不会选它。 - **安装器不再改你的 pip 配置。** `--auto-mirror` 以前会执行 `pip config set global.index-url`，改掉整台电脑上所有 Python 项目的下载源。现在选出的镜像只在这次安装中使用。 - **能看到模型存在哪里。** `installer.py --check` 和安装完成时，会列出正在使用的 Hugging Face 缓存及其中已有的模型、本地模型目录和 FFmpeg 的来源。新手指南说明了怎样复用已有的模型，包括单独安装的 WhisperX 的模型。 - 修复侧边栏对 TTS 服务商名称报 "Translation not found" 的问题。  ### ⚠️ 升级前留意  - **需要安装新依赖** `uharfbuzz`、`budoux` 和 `fonttools`：重新运行安装器，或执行 `pip install -r requirements.txt`。装好之前，字幕按原来的方式烧录，并提示重新运行安装器。 - **第一次烧录中文或日文字幕时需要联网**，下载对应字体（10–20 MB，只下载一次）。离线时，可以从 google/fonts 或 fonts.google.com 下载可变字体放进 `fonts/`，例如 `NotoSansSC[wght].ttf` 或 `NotoSansJP-VariableFont_wght.ttf`。 - **如果你的 `config.yaml` 里还是 `max_length: 75` 和 `target_multiplier: 1.2`**，请在「字幕长度微调」里点 **恢复默认值（20/80）**，改用新的默认值。 - `font_size` 现在是排版器原始字号的缩放比例（15 和 17 就是原始字号），排版器不使用 `margin_v`。填写 `font_name` 后，所有语言都不再使用排版器。 - 如果以前用 `--auto-mirror` 安装时改了全局 pip 下载源，可以用 `pip config list` 查看，用 `pip config unset global.index-url` 删除。  ### ✅ 发布验证  - 自动化测试：**832 passed，20 skipped**（macOS arm64、Python 3.12、安装器装的 FFmpeg）。 - 用 FFmpeg 和 libass 实际烧录，前后对比了中文、日文、韩文、俄文、阿拉伯文、印地文和泰文，包括排版器和退回的原滤镜两种情况。  Full Changelog / 完整改动：[v3.1.1...v3.2.0](https://github.com/Huanshere/VideoLingo/compare/v3.1.1...v3.2.0) 
```

### 2026-11-12T05:15:49Z [**kunkundi/crossdesk**](https://github.com/kunkundi/crossdesk)

```
**新增功能与改进：**  1. 新增 CrossDesk MCP，让 AI Agent 在授权下查看和操作远程电脑，支持截图、键鼠输入、剪贴板、文件传输、远端声音、多设备会话与多显示器操作。 2. 桌面端新增双栏文件浏览窗口，支持浏览本机与远端目录、双向传输文件和文件夹，并改善拖拽反馈与预览。 3. 新增最小化时暂停视频的可配置选项。 4. 新增远端输入执行确认、画面变化等待及桌面自动化能力，支持在具备相应能力的远端直接打开网址、启动应用、读取及操作可访问性元素。 5. 完善 Windows 安装版无人值守功能，后台服务与桌面客户端共用设备身份和连接密码，支持登录前远控及登录后的功能衔接。 6. Windows 主窗口与远控窗口采用独立任务栏分组，并改善 macOS 和 Linux 上的窗口切换、最小化及关闭行为。 7. 新增反馈与建议入口及诊断日志导出，可将日志与基本版本信息保存为 ZIP，方便反馈问题。 8. 新增远端显示器分辨率调整，支持 Windows、macOS 和 Linux X11 被控端；桌面和移动控制端可记住各设备、各显示器的选择，最后一个控制端断开后恢复原显示模式。 9. 支持在 Codex 右侧内置浏览器显示远端实时画面，并为 MCP Apps 提供兼容预览。 10. MCP 新增批量动作与 Agent 状态监看，减少重复调用；优化 Agent 光标样式与移动动画。 11. 新增 DeepSeek Harness 侧栏支持，并将插件、MCP 与原生程序合并为一个安装包。  **问题修复：**  1. 改善 MCP 会话稳定性，完善初始信令连接恢复与连接失败提示。 2. 修复部分 OpenGL 环境下的画面渲染异常，完善 NVDEC 运行时故障恢复及视频解码回退。 3. 修复窗口显示前定位不正确导致的闪烁。 4. Android 支持用户安装的 CA 证书，改善使用自签发证书的自托管连接。 5. 修复 Windows 键盘捕获恢复后的修饰键状态异常。 6. 修复 iOS 音频播放缓冲格式问题、较早系统版本上的连接崩溃；更新检查改为依据 App Store 已发布版本。 7. 改善 macOS 升级时的权限保留。 8. 修复 Windows 升级后的服务路径、开机自启状态及 Linux 自启动与连接异常；桌面端守护进程设置保存后即时生效。 9. 修复旧剪贴板缓存阻止远端写入的问题。 10. 修复 Windows 无显示器会话中的分辨率记录与恢复问题，避免后台服务阻塞系统关机。 11. 修复设备在线状态被心跳覆盖的问题。 12. 修复操作画面菜单时菜单意外关闭或误向远端发送输入的问题。 13. 修复显示器分辨率变化后的主窗口尺寸异常。 14. 修复网页会话后原生客户端连接可能崩溃的问题。 15. 修复不同控制传输方式下的网页端文件浏览兼容性。  **升级说明：**  1. iOS 最低支持版本为 **iOS 16.0**。GitHub 提供的 iOS 包未签名，需要自行签名安装；App Store 更新以商店实际发布情况为准。 2. Windows 登录前无人值守需要安装版 Release 客户端。Linux 远端分辨率调整需要 X11 和 RandR 支持，暂不支持 Wayland 或 XWayland 采集。 3. 本次同步发布 CLI、MCP 及 DeepSeek Harness 插件 **0.2.0**。使用新增自动化和分辨率控制功能时，建议将相关客户端同步升级到 **1.7.6**；实际可用功能取决于远端系统、权限和应用支持。  ---  **New Features and Improvements:**  1. Added CrossDesk MCP, allowing AI agents to view and control authorized remote computers, with screenshots, keyboard and mouse input, clipboard access, file transfers, remote audio, concurrent device sessions and multi-display operations. 2. Added a dual-pane desktop file browser for local and remote directories, with bidirectional file and folder transfers, improved drag feedback and previews. 3. Added a configurable option to pause video when minimized. 4. Added remote input acknowledgments, visual-change waits and desktop automation. Supported remote systems can open URLs, launch applications, and inspect or interact with accessibility elements directly. 5. Improved unattended access in installed Windows builds. The background service and desktop client share the same device identity and connection password, with remote access before login and user-session features after login. 6. Added separate Windows taskbar groups for the main and remote control windows, and improved window switching, minimization and closing behavior on macOS and Linux. 7. Added a feedback entry and diagnostic log export, allowing logs and basic version information to be saved as a ZIP archive for troubleshooting. 8. Added remote display resolution controls for Windows, macOS and Linux X11 hosts. Desktop and mobile controllers remember selections per device and display, while hosts restore the original display mode after the last controller disconnects. 9. Added live remote desktop views in the Codex built-in sidebar browser, with a compatible preview fallback for MCP Apps. 10. Added batched MCP actions and agent activity monitoring to reduce repeated tool calls, and improved the agent cursor appearance and movement animation. 11. Added DeepSeek Harness sidebar support and bundled the plugin, MCP and native runtime into a single installation package.  **Bug Fixes:**  1. Improved MCP session stability, initial signaling connection recovery and connection failure messages. 2. Fixed rendering issues in some OpenGL environments and improved recovery from NVDEC runtime failures and video decoder fallback. 3. Fixed window flicker caused by positioning windows after they became visible. 4. Added support for user-installed CA certificates on Android, improving connections to self-hosted servers using private certificate authorities. 5. Fixed incorrect Windows modifier states after keyboard capture recovery. 6. Fixed iOS audio playback buffer format issues and connection crashes on older system versions. Update checks now follow published App Store versions. 7. Improved macOS permission preservation during upgrades. 8. Fixed Windows service paths after upgrades, autostart state handling, and Linux autostart and connection issues. Desktop watchdog settings now take effect immediately after saving. 9. Fixed remote clipboard writes blocked by stale cache. 10. Fixed display resolution tracking and restoration in headless Windows sessions, and prevented background services from blocking system shutdown. 11. Fixed incorrect device presence updates from heartbeats. 12. Fixed video settings menus closing unexpectedly or forwarding unintended input to the remote computer. 13. Fixed incorrect main-window sizing after display resolution changes. 14. Fixed potential crashes when native clients connect after web sessions. 15. Fixed web file browsing compatibility across control transports.  **Upgrade Notes:**  1. The minimum supported iOS version is **iOS 16.0**. The iOS archive on GitHub is unsigned and requires your own signing. App Store update availability follows the actual store release. 2. Unattended access before Windows login requires an installed Release build. Remote resolution changes on Linux require X11 and RandR; Wayland and XWayland capture are currently unsupported. 3. CLI, MCP and the DeepSeek Harness plugin are released alongside this version as **0.2.0**. Upgrade the relevant clients to **1.7.6** to use the new automation and resolution controls. Available capabilities depend on the remote operating system, permissions and application support.  **完整变更 / Full Changelog：** [[v1.7.5-20261104…v1.7.6-20261112](https://github.com/kunkundi/crossdesk/compare/v1.7.5-20261104...v1.7.6-20261112)](https://github.com/kunkundi/crossdesk/compare/v1.7.5-20261104...v1.7.6-20261112) 
```

### 2026-11-17T01:12:49Z [**Fission-AI/OpenSpec**](https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec)

```
# OpenSpec 2.1  OpenSpec now supports global skills for coding agents. Install the skills once and your agent can use them in every project on your machine.  As more teams adopt Vercel’s `skills` installer, it’s becoming a common way to install skills across coding tools. OpenSpec is adopting it too, so we can spend less time maintaining installer code and more time improving the skills themselves.  The OpenSpec workflows themselves haven't changed.  ## What changed  - **Skills can be installed globally.** Vercel’s installer handles the location for each supported agent, whether it reads `~/.agents/skills/` or uses its own folder. - **`openspec update` asks where a project's skills should live.** In a repo that has its own OpenSpec skills, you choose once between keeping them in the repo and switching to global. Nothing changes until you choose. - **`openspec init --tools <ids>` in a new repo installs globally.** In 2.0 it wrote the skills into the repo. Add `--scope project` to keep them there. - **Skills are installed by Vercel’s `skills` package.** Installing or refreshing them needs Git and access to GitHub.  ## How to upgrade  Update the OpenSpec CLI, then run this in each project:  bash openspec update   In a repo that has its own OpenSpec skills, you'll see this choice:  text Where should OpenSpec keep this project’s skills?  ❯ Keep in this project   Keep the skills in this repo.   Switch to global       Install the skills globally, for every project on this machine.   **Keep in this project.** Press Enter. The skills stay in your repo. Commit `openspec/config.yaml`, which records your choice so your teammates aren’t asked. Choose this if GitHub’s hosted Copilot agent reads the skills from the repo. You can switch later with `openspec init --scope global`.  **Switch to global.** We recommend this for most teams. The skills are installed globally on your machine, and the copies in the repo are removed. Commit the removed files. Each teammate then pulls, runs `openspec update`, and answers **Yes** when it offers to set up skills on their machine. Upgrade everyone’s CLI before you switch a repo: earlier versions only know project skills.  In CI or from an agent there is no question: update refreshes the skills where they are.  **If a script sets up your repos,** add `--scope project` to keep new repos' skills in the repo:  bash openspec init --scope project --tools claude   **Note:** `openspec update` replaces the built-in skill folders. If you edited a built-in skill or added a file inside one, save it first. `openspec update --dry-run` shows what will change.  An npm install needs Node 22.20.0 or newer. The standalone executable needs nothing. If you already ran `npx skills add`, keep that install and run `openspec update`.  See the [upgrade guide](https://openspec.dev/docs/upgrade-2-1) for more details.  ## Fixes  - The Windows executables are now labeled as OpenSpec, published by FISSION AI INC. Their file properties used to name the build tool, and an antivirus product removed `openspec.exe` as a threat. If the download disappears or won't run, the installer now says antivirus is the likely cause and shows the npm install command.  **Full changelog:** [v2.0.0...v2.1.0](https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/compare/v2.0.0...v2.1.0) 
```

### 2026-11-16T18:43:47Z [**Gaurav-Gosain/tuios**](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios)

```
tuios 0.13.3 is a security and fix release. It closes four issues that @cr2007 reported privately, and I recommend it to everyone. It also fixes a regression from v0.13.2 in the focus keys, and a few bugs you reported.  Upgrading from v0.13.1 or later is live on macOS and Linux: install, then run `tuios daemon upgrade`, and every program in your panes keeps running.  bash brew install tuios                     # Homebrew (macOS, Linux) yay -S tuios-bin                       # AUR (tuios-web-bin for the web server) nix run github:Gaurav-Gosain/tuios     # Nix curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/main/install.sh | bash go install github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/cmd/tuios@v0.13.3 docker run -it --rm ghcr.io/gaurav-gosain/tuios:v0.13.3   A binary installed by the script or from an archive updates with `tuios update`.  ## Security  Thanks to @cr2007 for four careful reports, each with the lines and a way to reproduce it.  - **A removed paired device loses its access.** `tuios hosts remove DEVICE` now removes the key that `tuios pair` added to `~/.ssh/authorized_keys`, not only the `[hosts.DEVICE]` table. Before, the phone could still log in, and after a restart it got the default rights. The file is now written in one step, so a failed write cannot cut your own keys. A key pinned to a name with no table can do nothing, also on a link that is already open. If you removed a paired device before this release, run `tuios hosts remove DEVICE` again to remove its key. [GHSA-4j54-9xgf-cq7g](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/security/advisories/GHSA-4j54-9xgf-cq7g) ([#2162](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/2162)) - **Shell markers cannot fill the daemon's memory.** A program could print one OSC 133 prompt mark and many command marks, and each one read every line back to the prompt. The capture now stops at 64 lines and 16 KiB, and a repeated mark reuses the text it has. This is in `tuios.dev/vt` v0.3.3. [GHSA-w23r-7frv-qfhg](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/security/advisories/GHSA-w23r-7frv-qfhg) ([#2163](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/2163)) - **Programs in panes cannot take every daemon connection.** Panes, hooks and plugins together hold at most 896 of the 1024 connections, so 128 stay for your own client and `tuios` commands. One pane holds at most 64. A program in a pane also no longer draws on your own read budget. [GHSA-jv4p-w86m-gr3h](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/security/advisories/GHSA-jv4p-w86m-gr3h) ([#2170](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/2170)) - **Hooks and `exec` lines get a clean environment.** Daemon hooks, plugin commands, `exec` lines and task verify commands now start from the environment a pane gets. Before, a daemon started by `tuios restart-server` in a pane placed them in that pane, and they could act as it. [GHSA-v98m-7pr7-xv88](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/security/advisories/GHSA-v98m-7pr7-xv88) ([#2171](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/2171)) - The daemon decides whether a caller runs in one of its panes by the processes it started. Before, a pane program could pass as you after the daemon took over panes it did not start. ([#2142](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/2142))  ## Fixes  - Keys bound to `terminal_focus_up` and `terminal_focus_down` work again with the Neovim navigator. This broke in v0.13.2. Thanks to @5c077m4n for the report. ([#2161](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/2161)) - `tuios cp -m` no longer deletes the only copy when the source and the target are one file, through a link or on a disk that ignores case. Thanks to @cr2007. ([#2166](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/2166)) - A pane that runs an agent from a versioned interpreter, such as Hermes on `python3.14`, is detected again. Thanks to @sebapawlak. ([#2168](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/2168)) - With the omp v2 extension, the pane follows omp itself, so the agent state and a claimed task card stay right through a turn. Thanks to @sebapawlak. ([#2169](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/2169)) - The dock stops offering an undo when the undo window ends, and `u` names the window you set. Thanks to @Gsirawan. ([#2159](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/2159)) - `tuios run` waits for the first prompt in a fresh pane, and one slow frame no longer cuts the next workspace slide. ([#2154](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/2154)) - A host link follows the person's input, not the frames a client sends on its own. ([#2156](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/2156)) - A folder report from a pane no longer undoes a window move. ([#2146](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/2146)) - A new workspace takes the tiling mode of the one it was opened from. ([#2144](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/2144))  ## Compatibility  A v0.13.3 client and daemon work with v0.12 and v0.13 peers. Code paths for peers older than v0.12 are gone, so the binary is a little smaller. ([#2153](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/2153)) ([#2155](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/2155))  **Full changelog**: https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/compare/v0.13.2...v0.13.3  ---  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/compare/v0.13.2...v0.13.3 
```

### 2026-11-16T06:44:25Z [**longbridge/gpui-kit**](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit)

```
# GPUI Kit v0.10.0  GPUI Kit 0.10.0 is a minor release. It moves the optional GPUI Fast backend to 0.1.16, adds global right-to-left layout, an explicit reduced-motion preference, roving keyboard navigation in Tabs, opt-in match highlighting in Select / Combobox / Command, and a long run of input, text, table and accessibility fixes. Two small breaking changes in the input API make this a minor release.  ## Breaking Changes  - **Textarea**: `TextareaMode::Extras` is now `TextareaExtras` instead of `()`. Only code that names this associated type is affected. ([#3970](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3970))   diff   - type Extras = ();   + type Extras = TextareaExtras;    - **Input**: `EditorState::text_gutter_margin()` is replaced by `text_origin_offset() -> Option<Pixels>`. ([#3765](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3765)) - **GPUI Fast 0.1.16**: every `Element` now gets `IntoElement` from GPUI Fast itself. Apps built on the `gpui-fast` feature must drop their own `impl IntoElement for X { type Element = Self; ... }` on element types, or they will not compile. The default gpui-pre backend is unchanged.  ## New APIs  - **Layout**: global right-to-left layout direction. ([#3886](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3886)) - **Motion**: an explicit reduced-motion preference. ([#3881](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3881)) - **Select, Combobox, Command**: opt-in `highlight_matches`, with a new `list.highlight.foreground` theme color. ([#3959](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3959), [#3960](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3960)) - **Input**: `set_tokens` and text decorations on `TextareaState`, `context_menu_offset`, and the editor text origin offset. ([#3971](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3971), [#3970](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3970), [#3920](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3920), [#3765](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3765)) - **Table**: `TableRow::id`, `TableRow::hover`, and `DataTable::scrollbar_mode`. ([#3907](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3907), [#3912](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3912), [#3945](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3945)) - **Text**: `TextView::link` to replace or drop links, and an option to keep raw HTML in Markdown as text. ([#3743](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3743), [#3979](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3979)) - **Components**: `Checkbox::indeterminate`, `SidebarGroup::suffix`, `NumberInput` stepper option, `Diff::split_ratio`, `MessageScroller::scroll_to_offset`, `SelectableText` highlights, and tooltip anchor offsets. ([#3980](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3980), [#3982](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3982), [#3748](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3748), [#3883](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3883), [#3922](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3922), [#3929](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3929), [#3874](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3874)) - **Locales**: Japanese. ([#3976](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3976))  ## Editor and text input  - Move the caret and delete by grapheme cluster; reveal the caret after a long single-line paste; report a preedit kept by `unmark_text` as a change; keep IME candidates on the matching row. ([#3887](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3887), [#3967](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3967), [#3965](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3965), [#3939](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3939)) - Hit-test past the gutter, open completion menus above the caret when they do not fit below, let Copy with no selection reach the window selection, keep the tab indicator inside its tab. ([#3921](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3921), [#3925](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3925), [#3915](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3915), [#3983](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3983)) - Textarea: no newline on secondary Enter, and no scrolling at fractional scales while auto-growing. ([#3879](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3879), [#3882](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3882))  ## Markdown and text rendering  - Fold white space as HTML does, parse `<pre>` as a code block, lay out several images inline, and number list items correctly after a sub-list. ([#3949](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3949), [#3899](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3899), [#3898](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3898), [#3876](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3876)) - Code spans: count oversized insets once per line, keep leading white space beside a fold. ([#3972](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3972), [#3962](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3962)) - Selection: triple click selects a line or wrapped paragraph, drag auto-scroll starts at an edge that hides text, a press on a control keeps the selection, a cursor set on `TextView` replaces the I-beam. ([#3930](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3930), [#3942](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3942), [#3928](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3928), [#3951](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3951), [#3966](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3966), [#3927](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3927), [#3964](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3964))  ## Components  - **Table**: no per-frame redraw of a striped table that does not fill its area, and no per-row cross-axis measure. ([#3978](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3978), [#3868](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3868)) - **Tooltip**: animate width between tooltips, wrap long text within `max_w`, show one tooltip when nested in a native `.tooltip()`. ([#3957](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3957), [#3975](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3975), [#3896](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3896), [#3914](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3914)) - **Diff**: keep split columns beside a long line, keep the header in view when switching modes, collapse many files in one rebuild. ([#3958](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3958), [#3884](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3884), [#3935](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3935)) - **Button**: spinner on a loading button without icon, no focus on right/middle press, no hover style while disabled. ([#3908](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3908), [#3924](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3924), [#3943](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3943)) - **Select, Popover, Dialog, Dock, Settings, Slider, Attachment, Tabs, Title bar**: keep menu height while searching; keep a popover open for clicks in its child popup; offset stacked dialogs by open dialogs only; let the skin size a closed dock; fold the settings page from its title; slider keyboard shortcuts; animated GIF previews; roving Tabs navigation; keep a title-bar click that moves a pixel. ([#3944](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3944), [#3909](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3909), [#3897](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3897), [#3931](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3931), [#3947](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3947), [#3919](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3919), [#3903](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3903), [#3900](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3900), [#3918](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3918)) - **Window, WebView, Theme, Native menu**: end mouse gestures when the compositor eats MouseUp, redraw after releasing the composition surface, Windows scrollbar convention, reject bad hex digits instead of panicking, SVG sniffing across a split character. ([#3643](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3643), [#3986](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3986), [#3895](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3895), [#3936](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3936), [#3890](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3890))  ## Accessibility  - Expose single- and multi-line input text, labels and disabled inputs to assistive technology; numeric `SetValue` on Slider; a visible label on the copy button; Tab leaves selection controls that become disabled. ([#3818](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3818), [#3894](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3894), [#3937](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3937), [#3877](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3877), [#3963](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3963), [#3910](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3910), [#3872](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3872), [#3875](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3875))  ## Dependencies  - GPUI Fast 0.1.16; gpui-pre stays at 0.3.12.  ## Contributors  <details> <summary>Thank you to everyone who contributed to this release</summary>  @huacnlee, @madcodelife, @shiz92, @JackLuciano, @wangdiandao, @jeonsu0609, @yuluod, @nxtvoid, @kevin9327, @Bombatomica64, @ylinwind, @vinevard, @ualtinok, @Tunglies, @taoqf, @sodekim, @since2006, @shreeve, @sassman, @PanicIsReal, @mycodecrafting, @m-gebhardt, @Map1en, @kossoy, @iam4x, @Elitelord, @davonium, @costajohnt, @bet4it, @aetoskevin.  </details>  <details> <summary>What's Changed</summary>  * base: Turn on `test-support` for gpui-base's own tests by @shreeve in Tab exit when focused selection controls become disabled by @wangdiandao in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3872 * number_input: Add stepper option by @taoqf in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3876 * input: Report a disabled input as disabled to assistive technology by @Bombatomica64 in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3877 * ### 2026-11-11T18:09:58Z [**cokice/japanese-analyzer**](https://github.com/cokice/japanese-analyzer)

```
本次发布改进整句朗读与语音设置、弹窗和阅读界面，并包含 v0.4.0 之后的性能、错误提示与请求处理修复。  ## 朗读与语音设置  - Gemini 朗读升级至 `gemini-3.8-flash-lite-tts`，适配独立的朗读风格参数及 WAV 音频输出，保留原始 PCM 兼容处理。 - 修复语音设置菜单被解析文字遮挡、菜单按钮点击被正文拦截的问题。 - 在解析后的阅读工具栏中增加语音设置入口，编辑态和阅读态共用 Edge/Gemini 引擎、声音、语速和朗读风格选项。 - 语音菜单支持视口边缘避让、滚动定位、点击外部和 Esc 关闭。 - 移除「正在准备朗读／预计需要」提示块，保留朗读按钮加载状态和音频播放器。  ## 界面与交互  - 设置、登录、移动端词汇详情和展开聊天使用原生 dialog；语言选择使用 popover，并提供兼容处理。 - 修复聊天中输入法确认候选词的回车处理，保留确认操作的默认行为。 - 统一字号、圆角和动画变量，放大词汇详情正文，提高释义和例句的可读性。 - 改进深色模式下的原生控件、滚动条和输入框自适应高度。 - 修复保存设置后关闭弹窗可能重新打开的问题，清理未使用的组件和样式。  ## 性能与稳定性  - 延后加载字体样式并内联首屏 CSS，减少首页渲染阻塞。 - 补齐繁体中文、英文和韩文的 API 错误提示翻译。 - 增加登录尝试限流和请求体大小限制，修复异步请求取消、过期响应、输入与每日一句更新问题。 - Docker 与 CI 固定使用 Node.js 22.23.4。  ## 安装与升级  Docker 镜像：`howenhowen/japanese-analyzer:v0.5.0`，提供 `linux/amd64` 和 `linux/arm64`。  bash docker pull howenhowen/japanese-analyzer:v0.5.0   ## 验证  - 合并后的 master 与 v0.5.0 标签 CI 全部通过；amd64、arm64 镜像及多架构 manifest 已发布。[版本 CI](https://github.com/cokice/japanese-analyzer/actions/runs/43074290372)。  - 本地测试、ESLint、Next.js 生产构建和 diff 检查通过。 - Chromium 验证了菜单遮挡修复、编辑态/阅读态设置同步、朗读参数传递、键盘关闭、移动端视口和深色模式；相关 API 响应使用模拟数据。 - Safari/iOS 真机及真实系统输入法候选词操作未实测。  完整变更：[v0.4.0…v0.5.0](https://github.com/cokice/japanese-analyzer/compare/v0.4.0...v0.5.0)。 
```

### New - Generator: Added ImprovMX email forwarder support. - Generator: Added Migadu email forwarder support. - Generator: Added mailcow email forwarder support.  ### Improvements - UI: GPG and SSH settings screens now hide Custom and Misc filter sections. - Data: Updated translations.  ### Fixes - Android: Fixed Keystore outages so encrypted storage is not wiped and vault data is not treated as lost. - Android: Fixed Watchtower security checks remaining at 0 after an update or reinstall (#1687). - Desktop: Fixed a memory leak after usage (#1683). - GPG: Fixed being unable to import a GPG key when a child key password is different from the main key password (#1684). - UI: Fixed the monochrome tint color so it does not degrade to cyan (#1682). 
```

### 2026-11-17T12:51:31Z [**gildas-lormeau/SingleFile**](https://github.com/gildas-lormeau/SingleFile)

```
### Changes  - The unused CSS rules of the stylesheets found in shadow roots are now removed when the option "Stylesheets > remove unused styles" is enabled, they were always saved whole. A stylesheet shared by several components is pruned for each component (i.e. for the shadow roots whose hosts have the same tag name). On a Reddit thread with 240 comments, the saved page goes from 394 MB to 21 MB (#1514) - The deferred content is also loaded on a page that keeps the document at the size of the window and scrolls an inner element; such a page was saved with its first rows only - An SVG file referenced by a `use` element is inlined in the page: a reference to a whole file (`<use href="icon.svg">`) was written as a data URI that no browser draws, and a referenced fragment that is not a `symbol` was drawn a second time at its own position - In the annotation editor, a click on a link to an anchor of the page (`<a href="#...">`) scrolls to its target when no edit tool is active, the click was ignored (#2059)  ### Fixes  - A stylesheet containing `:is()` or `:where()` with an empty argument list no longer makes the save fail with "Cannot read properties of null (reading 'data')"; it was reported on github.com pages with Chrome 109 (#2058) - Saving a selection no longer gives a blank page when the selected content is displayed through the slots of web components: the shadow roots of the ancestors of the selection were emptied, slots included, and the shadow root of a selected component lost its content (#2060) - Updated single-file-core to 1.14.1 
```

## Release Notes  Released on 2026-11-12.  ### Preview features  - Redirect renamed rules ([#30081](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/30081)) - \[`flake8-async`\] Implement `pytest-raises-exception-group` (`ASYNC401`) ([#28772](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28772)) - \[`flake8-logging-format`\] Mark `G004` fix as unsafe ([#30087](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/30087)) - \[`pylint`\] Implement `no-method-argument` (`PLE0211`) ([#29748](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29748)) - \[`refurb`\] Do not offer fix when `write` is nested in an expression (`FURB103`) ([#29789](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29789)) - \[`ruff`\] Add `renamed-rule` (`RUF202`) ([#30079](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/30079)) - \[`ruff`\] Expand `redirected-noqa` to other suppression comments (`RUF101`) ([#30078](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/30078))  ### ### 2026-11-14T09:56:56Z [**Yaozhuwa/easy-typing-obsidian**](https://github.com/Yaozhuwa/easy-typing-obsidian)

```
## 更新内容  - 开启「增强代码块编辑」后，在行内代码中按 Cmd/Ctrl+A，可先选中内容，不包含两侧反引号。([#373](https://github.com/Yaozhuwa/easy-typing-obsidian/issues/373)) - 修复 Obsidian 1.15 中无法用 Tab 跳出行内代码的问题。 - 修复启用前缀词典后，粘贴中英文混合文本时严重卡顿的问题。([#378](https://github.com/Yaozhuwa/easy-typing-obsidian/issues/378)) - 修复输入法输入时误触发其他已打开笔记中的规则的问题。感谢 [GuiLuan](https://github.com/GuiLuan) 提交 [#372](https://github.com/Yaozhuwa/easy-typing-obsidian/pull/372)。 - 删除多余空行时，保留代码块内的空行和表格前的必要空行。([#349](https://github.com/Yaozhuwa/easy-typing-obsidian/issues/349)、[#375](https://github.com/Yaozhuwa/easy-typing-obsidian/issues/375)) - 修复带标题等附加信息的代码块无法切换注释、按语言匹配规则的问题。([#377](https://github.com/Yaozhuwa/easy-typing-obsidian/issues/377)) - 修复 `#a_code` 等标签被误判为代码或公式，导致错误加空格或匹配规则的问题。([#367](https://github.com/Yaozhuwa/easy-typing-obsidian/issues/367)) - 修复自定义正则区块对旧版默认配置、换行符、注释及行尾空白的误报。错误直接显示在设置项旁，插件加载时只提醒一次，输入时不再反复弹窗。([#379](https://github.com/Yaozhuwa/easy-typing-obsidian/issues/379))  ## Changes  - With **Better Code Edit** enabled, Cmd/Ctrl+A inside inline code selects its contents first, without the backticks. ([#373](https://github.com/Yaozhuwa/easy-typing-obsidian/issues/373)) - Restored Tab navigation out of inline code on Obsidian 1.15. - Fixed severe lag when pasting mixed Chinese/English text with a prefix dictionary. ([#378](https://github.com/Yaozhuwa/easy-typing-obsidian/issues/378)) - Fixed IME input in one editor triggering rules in another open note. Thanks to [GuiLuan](https://github.com/GuiLuan) for [#372](https://github.com/Yaozhuwa/easy-typing-obsidian/pull/372). - Deleting blank lines now preserves blank lines inside code blocks and the separator before tables. ([#349](https://github.com/Yaozhuwa/easy-typing-obsidian/issues/349), [#375](https://github.com/Yaozhuwa/easy-typing-obsidian/issues/375)) - Fixed comment toggling and language-scoped rules in code blocks with titles or other metadata. ([#377](https://github.com/Yaozhuwa/easy-typing-obsidian/issues/377)) - Fixed tags such as `#a_code` being mistaken for code or math, causing incorrect spacing or rule matching. ([#367](https://github.com/Yaozhuwa/easy-typing-obsidian/issues/367)) - Fixed custom regex configuration errors caused by legacy defaults, line endings, comments, and trailing whitespace. Errors now appear beside the setting, with one summary when the plugin loads, instead of repeated notices while typing. ([#379](https://github.com/Yaozhuwa/easy-typing-obsidian/issues/379))  ## 开发维护 / Development  - 更新 TypeScript、esbuild 和 Obsidian API 类型依赖，固定依赖锁文件，并增加回归测试。 - Updated TypeScript, esbuild, and Obsidian API types, committed a dependency lockfile, and added regression tests.  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/Yaozhuwa/easy-typing-obsidian/compare/6.0.9...6.0.10 
```

### 2026-11-17T14:02:35Z [**STranslate/STranslate**](https://github.com/STranslate/STranslate)

```
## 更新  - 添加：翻译前文本替换规则，支持纯文本、正则表达式、规则排序和草稿测试，适用于主窗口翻译、词典查询、替换翻译及图片翻译 @Fiveo9 #814 - 添加：输入区与历史记录支持替换差异高亮、悬停预览和按服务复制实际发送文本，历史 CSV 可选导出请求文本及语种 - 添加：精简图片翻译结果支持独立贴图，可选择复制文字、切换原图与译文，默认使用 Ctrl+T 贴图、Ctrl+/ 切换图片 @Lne27 #769 - 添加：主窗口贴顶自动收起，鼠标悬停顶部感应条即可展开，支持自定义收起延时 - 添加：乌克兰语界面及内置插件本地化 @pasharm #782 - 添加：音频播放静音预热设置，可按需调整预热时长，改善部分设备播放开头缺失的问题 @Tom-339 #813 - 添加：OCR 与图片翻译切换服务、语种或图片翻译分段方式后自动重新执行，支持分别开关 - 添加：增量翻译支持按住快捷键时自动清空原输入，可在设置中关闭 - 添加：划词取词失败支持仅发送托盘通知，保留当前输入与结果 - 优化：图片翻译默认使用精简窗口，可在设置中切换为独立窗口 - 优化：选中文本优先通过 UI Automation 读取，失败后自动回退到模拟复制，减少对剪贴板的占用 - 优化：主窗口显示时加入平滑上升与淡入动画 - 优化：切换 AI 提示词后默认自动重新翻译 - 优化：有道翻译 API 错误提示支持所有界面语言 - 修复：插件市场目录地址变更后无法正常加载插件列表 - 修复：启用托盘双击操作后，单击无法切换主窗口显示状态 - 修复：横向调整主窗口宽度后，窗口高度不再随内容自动适应 - 修复：主窗口被意外最大化 #804 - 修复：精简图片翻译窗口在不同缩放比例的副屏上位置或尺寸异常 @Lne27 #794 - 修复：模拟复制与粘贴时意外激活目标应用的 Alt 菜单，并改进快捷键发送的可靠性 @XFZAIMFQ #831 - 修复：MTranServer 插件错误提示不支持繁体中文 @NKid #820 - 修复：OpenAI 翻译默认不再发送 temperature 参数，并提供发送开关，兼容不支持该参数的模型 @Art-Zloader - 修复：从备份恢复配置时，备份文件被读取流占用导致恢复失败  ## 其他  - [插件市场](https://stranslate.zggsong.com/plugins/) - [使用说明](https://stranslate.zggsong.com/docs/) - [集成调用](https://stranslate.zggsong.com/docs/invoke/) - [安装卸载](https://stranslate.zggsong.com/docs/install/) - [FAQ](https://stranslate.zggsong.com/docs/faq/)  **完整更新日志:** [v2.0.10...v2.0.11](https://github.com/STranslate/STranslate/compare/v2.0.10...v2.0.11) 
```

- Made backup checks faster for libraries with many folders and reduced repeated album refreshes during backup. - Added a shortcut to the gallery’s Layout menu to show or hide photos shared with you. - Photo selection shortcuts now scroll in one row. Pull up to see the full menu. - Fixed cases where removing photos from the wrong person failed or an earlier correction was lost. - Fixed deleted people lingering in the People list until the app restarted. - Improved date matching in memories and fixed photos appearing out of order in shorter memories. - Fixed an issue on iOS where opening Ente after a background launch could skip the PIN or password lock. - Added a clearer message when a password sign-in attempt expires. - Improved on-device indexing for large JPEG XL photos and limited memory use when opening malformed Live Photos. - Made plan selection consistent between onboarding and subscription settings, and clarified what paid plans include in Ente Locker. - Animated the illustrations for empty album searches, Trash, Archive, and Links. - Fixed ML-related crashes on Mali GPUs

### 2026-11-17T12:37:31Z [**ypq123456789/TrafficCop**](https://github.com/ypq123456789/TrafficCop)

```
## 修复 issue #25：重置日每分钟重复推送「🔄 新周期开始」  **现象**：计费周期重置当天，从 0 点到 8 点每分钟都收到同一条「新周期开始」通知；有用户一早上收到 **485 条**。  **三重根因**：  1. `check_reset_limit()` 用「今天 == 周期起点」判断重置，重置日一整天都成立；cron 每分钟跑一次，就会写 1440 条重置日志。 2. vnstat 不为 **0 字节的小时**建记录。新周期刚开始时本周期无记录，旧逻辑误判为「取数失败」，导致后面的「流量正常」日志写不出来。 3. 通知脚本没有新周期去重；即使主脚本只写一条重置日志，通知侧也会在每分钟轮询时反复推送。  ## 修复内容  - **周期重置改为事件驱动**：当前周期起点落盘到 `.last_period_reset`。只有周期标识真正变化时才清限速、写一条重置日志；同一周期重复运行完全静默。首次升级只初始化标记，**不伪造通知、不删现有限速**。 - **通知去重按周期标识，不按状态文本**：每个渠道独立记录 `.last_new_cycle_notified_<channel>`；发送成功才写标记，失败保留到下一分钟重试。推送新周期的那一轮不再重复推「限速解除」。 - **区分「周期刚开始没流量」与「vnstat 停更」**：本周期无记录时，再校验 vnstat `updated` 日期；若最后更新早于周期起点，按取数失败处理，绝不把停更误当成 0 用量而解除限速。 - **季度 / 年度周期窗口修正**：改为按完整日期比较、周期长度计算锚点月、短月起始日夹到月末、结束日取「下个周期起点 - 1 天」。同时修掉 8/9 月被 bash 当八进制、7 月底跨 8 月初窗口为空等问题。   - 季度窗口：约 151 天 → **90 天**   - 年度窗口：约 730 天 → **365 天**  ## 升级注意  - 升级后**首次运行不会补发**新周期通知，这是刻意设计：避免把历史周期当成新事件。 - 季度 / 年度周期用户的用量读数会**一次性变小**，这是把错误窗口修正为正确窗口，不是数据丢失。此前多算的部分不会追扣，下个周期起恢复正常。 - 月度周期用户在大多数情况下读数不变；若恰好卡在 8/9 月或月末边界，读数会回到正确窗口。  ## 已知低风险边界  CodeRabbit 最终复审：`Merge Risk = Low`（覆盖 `6362bc5`），无阻塞意见。它记录的两个窄边界：  - 周期重置时 `tc qdisc del` 偶发失败，且 vnstat 同时持续取数失败，旧限速可能保留到取数恢复；本版不为此增加无限重试。 - 新周期刚跨日、vnstat 已标记更新但尚无本周期记录时，可能短暂按 0 用量处理；下一轮数据落盘后自愈。  ## 验证  - `test_period_reset.sh`：**69/69 PASS**（周期重置幂等、周期窗口、短月、全月扫描、8/9 月、7→8 月边界、三渠道事件通知、发送失败重试、渠道隔离、vnstat updated 解析、语法检查） - CodeRabbit 多轮审查逐条处理，最终 Low；CodeQL / Analyze (javascript-typescript) / GitGuardian 全绿 - 既有回归套件（crontab 原子化、日志轮转、cron 修复、进制默认值、状态快照、用量窗口）保持通过  **完整变更**：https://github.com/ypq123456789/TrafficCop/pull/26 
```

### 2026-11-12T02:31:10Z [**gogs/gogs**](https://github.com/gogs/gogs)

```
### Changed  - Lowering `[auth] ACTIVATE_CODE_LIVES` or `[auth] RESET_PASSWORD_CODE_LIVES` now also shortens the lifetime of activation, email confirmation, and password reset links already sent. [#8478](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8478)  ### Fixed  - _Security:_ On Windows hosts, a user creating a repository with initial files or initializing its labels could read files on the server, including the configuration file. [#8488](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8488) - [GHSA-fv2v-vwmj-j74p](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-fv2v-vwmj-j74p) - _Security:_ Referencing an issue in a commit message added a comment to it even when the pusher could not access its repository, letting anyone post into issues of private repositories. [#8486](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8486) - [GHSA-53f6-cgcf-gp6v](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-53f6-cgcf-gp6v) - _Security:_ Viewing an Org-mode file could show the contents of arbitrary files on the server, including the configuration file. [#8484](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8484) - [GHSA-hr26-3fq5-9365](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-hr26-3fq5-9365) - _Security:_ On Windows hosts, an authenticated user with write access could still write files into a repository's `.git` directory through other names for it, leading to code execution on the server. [#8483](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8483) - [GHSA-hc35-pcqw-5xjq](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-hc35-pcqw-5xjq) - _Security:_ A username containing HTML, which could come from an external login source such as LDAP, was rendered as markup on issue and pull request pages and in the dashboard issue and pull request lists. [#8482](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8482) - [GHSA-vhf5-f34f-vhq9](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-vhf5-f34f-vhq9) - _Security:_ Webhooks, repository migrations, and mirrors could reach services on the Gogs host through the IPv6 address `[::]`, bypassing the local network restriction. [#8481](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8481) - [GHSA-799r-mx22-r63q](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-799r-mx22-r63q) - _Security:_ Any signed-in user who knew the UUID of an attachment in another repository, including a private one, could move it to their own issue, comment, or release to download it, in some cases removing it from the original. [#8480](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8480) - [GHSA-p92p-7887-3g33](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-p92p-7887-3g33) - _Security:_ A password reset link could be used to sign in as the user without their password or two-factor authentication. Activation, email confirmation, and password reset links sent before upgrading no longer work. [#8472](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8472) - [GHSA-pmfh-wh5m-gmjm](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-pmfh-wh5m-gmjm) - _Security:_ Accounts with login prohibited or not activated kept repository access through access tokens, passwords, and SSH keys. [#8439](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8439) - [GHSA-f9cv-gfvg-3464](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-f9cv-gfvg-3464) - _Security:_ On Windows hosts, an authenticated user with write access could write files into a repository's `.git` directory through a crafted tree path. [#8408](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8408) - [GHSA-85m5-cfw6-3cx8](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-85m5-cfw6-3cx8) - _Security:_ Argument injection through a crafted commit or branch reference on several repository API endpoints allowed an authenticated user with read access to leak internal server details to the error logs. [#8393](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8393) - [GHSA-mxrh-2rxr-6mqc](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-mxrh-2rxr-6mqc) - _Security:_ Argument injection through a crafted branch name when creating a pull request allowed an authenticated user with write access to write files to arbitrary paths on the server. [#8390](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8390) - [GHSA-2grc-qr7q-6m36](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-2grc-qr7q-6m36) - Organization member and team actions, such as removing a member or leaving a team, failed with "Bad Request: no CSRF token present". [#8399](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/issues/8399)  <details> <summary>Previous patch releases</summary>  ## 0.14.3  ### Fixed  - _Security:_ Reverse proxy authentication header was honored from any remote address, allowing user impersonation when Gogs was reachable directly. The header is now only trusted from addresses listed in `[auth] TRUSTED_PROXY_IPS`. [#8264](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8264) - [GHSA-w6j9-vw59-27wv](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-w6j9-vw59-27wv) - _Security:_ Server-side request forgery in webhook deliveries via HTTP redirects to local network addresses. [#8263](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8263) - [GHSA-c4v7-xg93-qf8g](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-c4v7-xg93-qf8g) - _Security:_ Denial of service when rendering issue references against a malformed external issue tracker URL format. [#8312](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8312) - [GHSA-4j89-2c4f-44c6](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-4j89-2c4f-44c6) - _Security:_ Stored XSS in Jupyter notebook (`.ipynb`) preview through Markdown links with `javascript:` URLs. [#8319](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8319) - [GHSA-jq8v-rmf6-65jw](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-jq8v-rmf6-65jw) - _Security:_ Missing authorization check on the attachment download endpoint allowed anyone who knew (or guessed) an attachment UUID to download files belonging to private repositories. [#8320](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8320) - [GHSA-p9f5-h3rx-j5qw](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-p9f5-h3rx-j5qw) - _Security:_ Organization team and member management actions accepted GET requests, allowing a logged-in owner to be tricked into adding an attacker to the Owners team via a crafted link. [#8321](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8321) - [GHSA-pwx3-qcgw-vh7h](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-pwx3-qcgw-vh7h) - _Security:_ SSRF via mirror address update bypassing clone address validation. [#8225](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8225) - [GHSA-wv27-2vqp-j7g5](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-wv27-2vqp-j7g5) - _Security:_ Open redirect on login and other post-action flows via the `redirect_to` query parameter. [#8322](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8322) - [GHSA-xxhq-69mf-w8cr](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-xxhq-69mf-w8cr) - _Security:_ Privilege escalation to repository owner via collaboration access mode update. [#8227](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8227) - [GHSA-4565-r4x7-hg8j](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-4565-r4x7-hg8j) - _Security:_ SSRF in repository migration and recurring mirror sync via HTTP redirects and stale host validation on stored mirror URLs. [#8324](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8324) - [GHSA-g2f5-gjr4-qjvm](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-g2f5-gjr4-qjvm) - _Security:_ Remote command execution via pull request rebase merges with crafted branch names. [#8301](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8301) - [GHSA-qf6p-p7ww-cwr9](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-qf6p-p7ww-cwr9) - _Security:_ Stored XSS in the milestone dropdown on the new issue page via crafted milestone names. [#8325](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8325) - [GHSA-vcm5-gvmp-78mp](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-vcm5-gvmp-78mp) - _Security:_ Stored XSS in Jupyter notebook (`.ipynb`) preview through `data:text/html` URIs that bypassed the sanitizer. [#8326](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8326) - [GHSA-3w28-36p9-w929](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-3w28-36p9-w929) - _Security:_ Write-level collaborators could change admin-only repository settings (issue tracker, wiki, mirror sync) via API. [#8327](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8327) - [GHSA-268j-37xf-pp52](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-268j-37xf-pp52) - _Security:_ Password reset tokens stayed valid for the account-activation lifetime, ignoring `[auth] RESET_PASSWORD_CODE_LIVES`. [#8328](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8328) - [GHSA-5c3f-6486-3g7g](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-5c3f-6486-3g7g) - _Security:_ Stored XSS in Jupyter notebook (`.ipynb`) preview through raw HTML in markdown cells. [#8330](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8330) - [GHSA-6vxv-wg6j-5qwp](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-6vxv-wg6j-5qwp) - _Security:_ Read-only Git HTTP access could be confused with write access during repository pushes. [#8331](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8331) - [GHSA-wmfg-5p4h-5fw3](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-wmfg-5p4h-5fw3) - _Security:_ Arbitrary file write outside the repository working tree via crafted upload filename routed through a committed directory symlink. [#8332](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8332) - [GHSA-89mr-xqfv-758m](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-89mr-xqfv-758m) - _Security:_ Cross-repository disclosure of Git LFS object contents by binding a known OID to another repository without proving possession of the bytes. [#8333](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8333) - [GHSA-6p9m-q3jp-47h4](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-6p9m-q3jp-47h4) - _Security:_ Remote code execution via path traversal in organization names accepted through the API. [#8334](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8334) - [GHSA-c39w-43gm-34h5](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-c39w-43gm-34h5) - _Security:_ Stalled SSH handshakes pinned a file descriptor and goroutine indefinitely. The built-in SSH server now drops connections that do not complete the handshake within 15 seconds. [#8335](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8335) - [GHSA-xp79-5mx3-jx52](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-xp79-5mx3-jx52) - _Security:_ Organization metadata and team list endpoints were reachable without authentication. [#8336](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8336) - [GHSA-744x-3838-5r56](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-744x-3838-5r56)  ## 0.14.2  ### Fixed  - _Security:_ Denial of service in repository and wiki file listing pages via crafted file names. [#8116](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8116) - [GHSA-3qq3-668m-v9mj](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-3qq3-668m-v9mj) - _Security:_ Cross-repository LFS object overwrite via missing content hash verification. [#8166](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8166) - [GHSA-gmf8-978x-2fg2](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-gmf8-978x-2fg2) - _Security:_ Stored XSS via data URI in issue comments. [#8174](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8174) - [GHSA-xrcr-gmf5-2r8j](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-xrcr-gmf5-2r8j) - _Security:_ Release tag option injection in release deletion. [#8175](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8175) - [GHSA-v9vm-r24h-6rqm](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-v9vm-r24h-6rqm) - _Security:_ Stored XSS in branch and wiki views through author and committer names. [#8176](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8176) - [GHSA-vgvf-m4fw-938j](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-vgvf-m4fw-938j) - _Security:_ DOM-based XSS via issue meta selection on the issue page. [#8178](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8178) - [GHSA-vgjm-2cpf-4g7c](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-vgjm-2cpf-4g7c) - Unable to update files via web editor and API. [#8184](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8184)  ### Removed  - Support for passing API access tokens via URL query parameters (`token`, `access_token`). Use the `Authorization` header instead. [#8177](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8177) - [GHSA-x9p5-w45c-7ffc](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-x9p5-w45c-7ffc)  ## 0.14.1  ### Added  - Support comparing tags in addition to branches. [#6141](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/issues/6141) - Show file name in browser tab title when viewing files. [#5896](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/5896) - Support using TLS for Redis session provider using `[session] PROVIDER_CONFIG = ...,tls=true`. [#7860](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/7860) - Support expanading values in `app.ini` from environment variables, e.g. `[database] PASSWORD = ${DATABASE_PASSWORD}`. [#8057](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8057) - Support custom logout URL that users get redirected to after sign out using `[auth] CUSTOM_LOGOUT_URL`. [#8089](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8089) - Start publishing next-generation, security-focused Docker image via `gogs/gogs:next-latest`, which will become the default image distribution (`gogs/gogs:latest`) starting 0.16.0. While not all container options support have been added in the next-generation image, the use of current legacy Docker image is deprecated, it will be published as `gogs/gogs:legacy-latest` starting 0.16.0, and be completely removed no earlier than 0.17.0. [#8061](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8061)  ### Changed  - The required Go version to compile source code changed to 1.25. - The build tag `cert` has been removed, and the `gogs cert` subcommand is now always available. [#7883](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/7883) - Switched to pure-Go SQLite driver, CGO is no longer required to compile Gogs. [#7882](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/issues/7882) - Updated Mermaid JS to 11.9.0. [#8009](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8009) - Halt the repository creation and leave the directory untouched if the repository root already exists. [#8091](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8091)  ### Fixed  - _Security:_ Unauthenticated file upload. [#8128](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8128) - [GHSA-fc3h-92p8-h36f](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-fc3h-92p8-h36f) - _Security:_ Protected branch bypass in web UI. [#8124](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8124) - [GHSA-2c6v-8r3v-gh6p](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-2c6v-8r3v-gh6p) - _Security:_ Authorization bypass allows cross-repository label modification. [#8123](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8123) - [GHSA-cv22-72px-f4gh](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-cv22-72px-f4gh) - _Security:_ Cross-repository comment deletion. [#8119](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/pull/8119) - [GHSA-jj5m-h57j-5gv7](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/security/advisories/GHSA-jj5m-h57j-5gv7) - 500 error on repository watchers and stargazers pages when using MSSQL. [#5482](https://github.com/gogs/gogs/issues/5482) - Submodules using `ssh://` protocol and a port number are not rendered correctly. ### 2026-11-13T09:30:24Z [**QuantumNous/new-api**](https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api)

```
> [!IMPORTANT] > 修复 v1.0.0-rc.50 在有 AES-NI、没有 AVX 的 x86-64 CPU 上崩溃并反复重启的问题（#8314）。Celeron、Pentium、Atom 一类 CPU（如 J4125）和只提供 AES、不提供 AVX 的虚拟机会受影响：处理聊天等请求时进程因 SIGILL 退出，模型列表和管理后台仍能打开，看起来像间歇性的 502。在这类机器上运行 rc.50 的请升级到本版本。 > > 本版本包含 rc.50 新增的提供商插件、请求处理插件和社区插件市场。提供商插件把一个上游厂商接成一种渠道；请求处理插件在请求和回复上运行，可以按全局、分组、渠道和模型生效。从 rc.49 或更早版本升级时请注意下面几点。 > > 原敏感词设置在首次启动时迁成一个「请求过滤」实例，旧设置项会被删除。命中时返回 400 `request_rejected`，原来是 500 `sensitive_words_detected`。回滚到旧版本后敏感词检查是关闭的，需要重新填写。 > > 多节点部署：设置、权限、插件、渠道等手动改动几秒内同步到所有节点。`CHANNEL_UPDATE_FREQUENCY` 的定时余额更新改为只在主节点运行。超过 30 天的已结束系统任务会被自动删除，需要保留更久的请先在 系统设置 → 运维 → 日志维护 里改保留天数。 > > Vertex AI 插件 1.3.1 起，视频请求发到渠道填的部署地区，不再固定发到 `global`。升级前请确认所用的视频模型在该地区可用。 > > Fixes v1.0.0-rc.50 crashing and restarting over and over on x86-64 CPUs that have AES-NI but not AVX (#8314). Celeron, Pentium and Atom parts (such as the J4125) and virtual machines that offer AES but not AVX are affected: the process exits with SIGILL while handling chat and similar requests, while the model list and the admin console still open, so it looks like an intermittent 502. If you run rc.50 on such a machine, upgrade to this release. > > This release includes the provider plugins, request processing plugins and community plugin marketplace added in rc.50. A provider plugin turns an upstream vendor into a channel type. A request processing plugin runs on requests and replies, scoped globally or to groups, channels and models. When upgrading from rc.49 or earlier, note the following. > > On first start, the old sensitive word setting becomes a Request filter instance and the old setting is removed. A match now returns 400 `request_rejected` instead of 500 `sensitive_words_detected`. After rolling back to an older version, sensitive word checks are off until you fill them in again. > > Multi-node deployments: manual changes to settings, permissions, plugins, channels and more reach every node within seconds. Scheduled balance updates from `CHANNEL_UPDATE_FREQUENCY` now run on the master node only. Finished system tasks older than 30 days are deleted automatically. To keep them longer, change the retention days in System Settings → Operations → Log Maintenance first. > > From Vertex AI plugin 1.3.1, video requests go to the channel's deployment region instead of always to `global`. Before upgrading, check that the video models you use are available in that region.  ## 中文  * **修复**：修复了 rc.50 在有 AES-NI、没有 AVX 的 x86-64 CPU 上处理请求时崩溃的问题（#8314）。JSON 库 MoeForge moego 升级到 v0.1.0-alpha.3，这类 CPU 改用 SSE 编码的 AES 指令，结果与其他 CPU 相同。 * **插件**：提供商插件可以接入路由、协议转换、模型列表、需要登录的上游（登录与令牌刷新）、余额查询和定时维护。请求处理插件可以试运行，每处修改记入使用日志；内置请求过滤、请求脱敏、全局参数覆盖、自动推理强度、图片压缩和内容审核。插件市场新增社区来源，官方和社区插件都能按 8 位插件码安装，安装时显示使用说明和更新日志；插件下载站点可以选主站或备用站。 * **多节点**：设置、权限、插件、登录方式、模型、订阅套餐和渠道的手动改动在各节点几秒内生效，系统信息页能看到同步进度和加载失败的节点。系统任务记录每天自动清理，默认保留 30 天。「更新全部余额」改为后台任务，列表随进度刷新。 * **计费**：图片张数按最终发给上游的请求计算，包括 SiliconFlow 的 `batch_size`、Imagen 的 `sampleCount`（不填按 4 张）和 Replicate 的 `num_outputs`。插件或渠道覆盖改了张数，预扣也跟着变。 * **支付**：易支付的签名方式可以选 MD5 或 RSA。 * **其他**：注册、修改密码、初始化向导和管理员编辑用户时会显示密码强度、长度要求和加强建议，只有长度不符合要求时才不能提交。新增 `HOST` 环境变量设置监听地址。DeepSeek 渠道的 Chat Completions 请求会把 developer 角色转成 system。修复了透传请求重试时，新渠道可能收到残缺请求体的问题。修复了切换渠道后，模型重定向同步把上一个渠道的模型名加进当前渠道的问题。修复了用输入法输入风险确认文字后按钮仍不可用的问题。修复了渠道插件设置里过长的内容把设置区撑出渠道编辑抽屉的问题。登录页的协议同意文字现在会翻译。插件运行时升级到 MoeForge moejs v0.1.0。  ## English  * **Fix**: Fixed the problem of rc.50 crashing while handling requests on x86-64 CPUs that have AES-NI but not AVX (#8314). The JSON library MoeForge moego is now v0.1.0-alpha.3, which uses the SSE encodings of the AES instructions on such CPUs, with the same results as on other CPUs. * **Plugins**: Provider plugins can bring routes, protocol conversion, model lists, upstreams that need sign-in (login and token refresh), balance queries and scheduled maintenance. Request processing plugins can be dry-run, and every change they make is recorded in the usage log. Built-in ones cover request filtering, request redaction, global parameter overrides, automatic reasoning effort, image compression and content moderation. The marketplace adds a community source. Official and community plugins can be installed by their 8-digit plugin code, and the install dialog shows the usage guide and changelog. Administrators choose the main or backup download site. * **Multi-node**: Manual changes to settings, permissions, plugins, login methods, models, subscription plans and channels take effect on every node within seconds. The system info page shows sync progress and nodes that failed to load a change. System task records are cleaned up daily and kept for 30 days by default. Update All Balances runs as a background task, and the list refreshes as it progresses. * **Billing**: Image counts come from the final request sent upstream, including SiliconFlow `batch_size`, Imagen `sampleCount` (4 when omitted) and Replicate `num_outputs`. When a plugin or channel override changes the count, the pre-charge follows it. * **Payment**: Epay can sign with MD5 or RSA, chosen in its Signature type setting. * **Also**: Sign-up, password change, the setup wizard and the admin user editor show password strength, the length requirement and tips for a stronger password. Only the length blocks submission. Added the `HOST` environment variable to set the listen address. DeepSeek channels map the developer role to system in Chat Completions requests. Fixed the problem of a retried pass-through request sending an incomplete body to the next channel. Fixed the problem of model redirect sync adding the previous channel's model names after switching channels. Fixed the problem of the risk confirmation button staying disabled after typing the confirmation text with an input method. Fixed the problem of long content in a channel's plugin settings pushing the settings out of the channel drawer. The legal consent text on sign-in pages is now translated. The plugin runtime is now MoeForge moejs v0.1.0.  ---  ## What's Changed * chore(web-deps): upgrade frontend dependencies by @QuentinHsu in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/6999 * perf(password): improve password validation and feedback by @QuentinHsu in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/6928 * perf(profile): restructure notification settings layout by @QuentinHsu in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/6955 * fix(web): align TanStack router devtools with the router core by @Calcium-Ion in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/8320 * fix(web): keep channel plugin settings inside the drawer by @seefs001 in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/8323 * fix: 升级 moego 到 v0.1.0-alpha.3，修复无 AVX 的 x86-64 CPU 上的 SIGILL 崩溃 by @Calcium-Ion in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/8322   rc.50 的变更列表 / rc.50 change list: https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/releases/tag/v1.0.0-rc.50  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/compare/v1.0.0-rc.50...v1.0.0-rc.51
