### 2026-11-17T14:46:26Z [**open-webui/open-webui**](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui)

```
 ### Added  - **Bring your own computer with Tether.** Tether turns your own computer into part of Open WebUI: models from Ollama, LM Studio or any OpenAI-compatible server, OpenAPI and MCP tool servers and Open Terminal servers that you add in the Tether app now show up just for you in the model selector, the Tools list of the + menu and the terminal menu of any Open WebUI you connect, from whatever device you open it on, and Open WebUI reaches them through Tether, so their addresses, commands and keys never leave your computer. This needs Tether 0.1.14 or later, or 0.1.16 for MCP servers, tool servers and terminals need "ENABLE_TOOL_SERVERS" turned on, and a Tether model whose ID matches a model already in Open WebUI stays hidden. Tether replaces Direct Connections. [#34397](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34397) - **Sandboxed agents on your own computer.** Tether 0.1.13 or later can lock the terminal on your computer into a sandbox, so models and coding agents work in your real files without reaching the rest of your machine: on macOS and supported Windows devices, Open Terminal and everything it launches, coding agents included, can only touch the one folder you pick, run offline unless you turn on Network access and accept no incoming connections, with no Docker, virtual machine or administrator setup needed. If the sandbox cannot be enforced, the terminal stays stopped instead of quietly running without it, and browser and desktop control stay off while it is on. Turn it on in Tether under Settings > Open Terminal > Sandbox. - **Local MCP servers through Tether.** Open WebUI can now use MCP servers that run as a local command over stdio, the way many MCP servers are distributed, by launching them on your computer through Tether 0.1.16 or later, with no proxy such as mcpo and no port to open. Add one in Tether under Settings > Integrations > Tools with its command, or connect an MCP server by its web address, and pick it from the Tools list of the + menu. Local commands run with your own account's access, outside Tether's workspace sandbox. - **A computer for every user.** With Terminals 0.4.0 or later connected, Open WebUI now hands out sandboxed computers on demand: anyone with Write access to the connection can create a terminal right from the terminal menu of the message input, pick whether everyone shares one machine or each user, chat or model gets its own, and set its image, CPU, memory, storage, network profile, environment variables, start directory and a setup script that, for example, clones a repository and installs its dependencies, without an administrator setting up each one. Terminals can be shared with users and groups and their instances started, stopped, restarted or deleted, a View files option opens any terminal's files without selecting it for the chat, System info shows how the setup script went, and those who manage a terminal can open the files and system info of other people's running instances. Start and Stop need Terminals 0.4.1 or later, the start directory and setup script need Open Terminal 0.17.7 or later in the terminal's image, and network profiles are the ones an administrator defined with "TERMINALS_NETWORK_PROFILES". - **Coding agents as chat models.** Coding agents now work much like any other model in the chat. Hermes, Antigravity, GitHub Copilot, Goose and Qwen Code join Codex, Claude Code, Cursor, Grok, OpenCode, Cline, Gemini CLI and Pi, and any other ACP agent added to Open Terminal with "OPEN_TERMINAL_ACP_AGENTS" works as well, once it is installed and signed in on the terminal. Auto for tool permissions now lets Codex, Claude Code and Grok approve routine actions themselves and pass on only what they escalate, and the reasoning effort in the advanced parameters now also applies to Claude Code, Pi and ACP agents that offer effort levels, such as OpenCode, which stop with an error when the chosen level is not offered for the model. Replies can now be regenerated and earlier messages edited, and a forked or cloned agent chat picks up where the original left off: Codex continues its own conversation from that point, other agents start over from the chat so far, and file changes the agent already made stay. Claude Code continues its own conversation too with the next Open Terminal release after 0.17.20 and until then asks you to update Open Terminal instead. The new agents and Auto for Claude Code and Grok need Open Terminal 0.17.19 or later, and effort for Claude Code, Pi and ACP agents needs 0.17.9 or later. [#34167](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34167) - **Pages open as tabs.** Notes, channels, the workspace, automations, the calendar, the playground, the home page and new chats from pinned models now open as tabs beside the current chat when you click or drag them from the sidebar, pick them in the user menu or follow a link to one of them in a chat message, and starting a new chat, also from a sidebar folder, keeps the open tabs. - **Queued messages steer replies.** A message you send to your own chat while a reply is still running now waits on the server, so it is kept when you leave the page, and the reply wraps up at its next step so your message is answered right away with what it has done so far. - **Instant first message.** The first message of a new chat now shows up right away while the chat is still being created, instead of only once it exists, unless files are still being uploaded with it. - **Full chat in side chats.** Side chats now run the same chat as the main view, so message buttons, sources, token usage, follow-up suggestions, slash commands, queued messages, chat variables, web search settings, tool events such as code execution and input requests, HTML and SVG previews, notifications while the window is in the background, unsent text after a page reload and answers from several models at once now work there too, and a side chat's tab now has the chat menu. [#34064](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34064), [#34165](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34165), [#34147](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34147), [#34031](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34031) - **Interactive visualizations.** Models can now draw interactive charts, diagrams, calculators and explainers right in the chat with the new Visualizations built-in tool, which is off by default and turned on per model under Builtin Tools, and administrators can rewrite its instructions under Admin Settings > Interface > Visualization Prompt or with "VISUALIZATION_PROMPT_TEMPLATE". - **Video generation.** Models can now generate videos with a built-in tool, also starting from an image in the chat, once an administrator turns on Video Generation under Admin Settings > Videos, adds connections to OpenAI, LiteLLM or OpenRouter, which offers models such as Veo and Kling, with a default video model, per-model defaults and access settings, and grants the Video Generation permission, which is off by default, after which users turn on Video in the + menu, can pick the model there and watch the result in the chat. [#13780](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/discussions/13780), [#13927](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/discussions/13927) - **Several image models.** Administrators can now add several image connections under Admin Settings > Images, each an OpenAI-compatible server such as LiteLLM, Gemini, ComfyUI or AUTOMATIC1111 with its own models, sizes, workflows and access, used for generating images, editing them or both, where OpenAI-compatible ones offer the same sign-in options and custom headers as model connections and list the image models of a LiteLLM server by themselves when no model IDs are entered, choose the default generation and edit models and give a model its own default image model in its settings, and users with more than one image model pick one from the Image entry in the + menu of the message input. [#10344](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/discussions/10344) - **Git panel becomes a GitHub workbench.** With Open Terminal 0.17.8 or later and the GitHub CLI signed in on the terminal, as included in the full, agents and cuda images, the Git panel now covers the whole GitHub loop: browse, search, create, edit, comment on, close and reopen the repository's issues, see each pull request's checks, review state, labels, activity and conflicts with a status filter and a choice of merge method, and search issues and pull requests across the whole repository instead of only the loaded list. Buttons such as Work on this, Fix, Address feedback and Resolve conflicts in chat hand the issue, review or conflict to the model as a ready-made request in the message input, and changed files can be picked with Ctrl or Shift click to discard several at once or all of them. - **Terminals follow your chats.** Each chat now saves its terminal and working folder and switches back to them when you reopen it, including a terminal you turned off, and administrators can pick a default terminal under Admin Settings > Models > Model Defaults that new chats start with for every model without a terminal of its own, so every chat can start with a computer attached. Chats from earlier versions open without a terminal unless they are coding agent chats. - **Folder chat board.** Folder pages now list their chats with a search through titles and messages and filters for status, tags, people, activity and last update, and can show them as a board with columns and rows by status, tag or activity such as unread, generating or waiting for approval, gives each chat there the sidebar's chat menu to rename, move, archive or delete it, and a folder's badge in the sidebar now also counts chats waiting for approval. [#33771](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33771) - **Chat statuses in folders.** Folders can now set up statuses for their chats under Edit statuses, with Backlog, To do, In progress, Waiting and Done offered as suggestions, chats then get a status from their menu on the folder page or by dragging them between board columns, people who can edit a shared folder can set the status of every chat in it, and each chat shows how long it has been in its status. - **Folder automations.** Folder pages now have an Automations section that lists the folder's automations with their state and last run and creates new ones there, chats made by an automation are marked with a clock, and automations can now post into shared folders their creator can edit, where everyone who can edit the folder can pause and run them and see their runs. - **Knowledge on folder pages.** Folder pages now have a Knowledge section that lists the knowledge bases, notes, chats and files new chats in the folder can use, where you can add, upload, drop, open and remove them. - **Colored chat tags.** Tags can now get a color on the folder page that applies wherever the tag is used, and the chat menu has a Tags submenu to find, add and create tags, and people who can edit a shared folder can tag every chat in it. - **Edit automations in place.** An automation's page now edits its name, instructions, status, schedule, model, terminal and destination right there with a Save button that appears once something changed, shows saved instructions collapsed behind Show more, lists its runs as activity below, and has Copy link and Duplicate in its More menu. - **Readable agent and terminal steps.** Steps of coding agents and the terminal tool calls of other models now show in the chat as what they did, a command with its output and its exit code when it failed, a diff of each edited file with its added and removed lines, or the files read, code and web searches, folders listed and pages fetched, and a group of steps is summarized as for example Ran 2 commands, Edited 1 file, Read 3 files. Diffs of files that other models edit through the terminal tools need Open Terminal 0.17.20 or later. - **Models can mention people.** With the Chat History built-in tool, models can now look up users by name or email and mention them in a reply, and each mentioned user who can see the chat gets an inbox notification that opens the chat at that reply. - **OpenAI-compatible vector stores.** External knowledge sources under Admin Settings > Integrations can now be OpenAI-compatible vector stores on OpenAI, Azure OpenAI or LiteLLM, searched on the server so no matching embedding model is needed, and you either pick one or more stores for a knowledge base or turn on 'List stores as knowledge bases' to show every store of the connection, or only chosen ones, as read-only knowledge bases that stay in sync with the endpoint and share the connection's access settings. - **Review settings before saving.** Admin settings pages now show next to Save how many settings changed since they were loaded or saved, and clicking that count or Save lists every change with its old and new value to confirm, with passwords and API keys kept hidden. [#34534](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34534), [#34532](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/discussions/34532) - **Smoother replies with code blocks.** Long replies with many code blocks no longer stall the tab a little longer with every new code block while they stream, so a reply with 300 code blocks that arrives at once now shows in 9 seconds instead of more than two minutes. [#34495](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34495), [#34492](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34492) - **Faster large PDF previews.** Long PDFs in the file preview now open in about a second and use far less memory, because only the pages near the screen are drawn and pages far away are released, so a 1500-page manual no longer takes over half a minute and a large scanned file no longer pushes the browser past many gigabytes. The browser's find (Ctrl+F) now only matches text on pages near the screen. [#34602](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34602), [#29576](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/29576) - **Faster folder reopening.** Reopening a folder now shows its chats, details and coding agent models right away from what was already loaded and refreshes them in the background, sidebar folders keep their loaded chats when collapsed and expanded again, and folder chat lists update immediately when a chat is renamed, moved, archived or deleted. - **Faster model list with Redis.** With Redis, the model list now loads faster and Redis no longer keeps a copy of every model profile image, which helps most with large animated or imported images, and Functions and Tools now receive the model without its profile image. [#34595](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34595), [#34594](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34594) - **Faster profile pictures and knowledge exports.** Large profile pictures of users, models and channel webhooks and knowledge base exports are now sent in one piece, so a 2 MB picture loads in about 15 ms instead of a few hundred and a page full of large avatars no longer takes seconds, and exporting a knowledge base no longer holds up other users' requests on the same server process while the archive is packed. [#34598](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34598), [#34597](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34597), [#34600](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34600), [#34599](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34599) - **Shared chat terminals.** People who can reply to a chat shared with Allow replies can now work in that chat's terminal, as the share dialog now points out: with a Terminals template that gives each chat its own machine, and in coding agent chats, they work in the same machine and files as the owner, while with other terminals they use their own access to it. [#34103](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34103) - **Files tab upgrades.** With a terminal that reports file changes, the Files tab now updates by itself without a loading spinner when files in the folders it shows change, also through commands run outside the chat. Clicking the path above it lets you type a folder to go to, absolute or relative to the current one, with Tab completing folder names, or select and copy an open file's full path, Markdown files get an Outline button that lists their headings and jumps to the one you pick, and folders in the working directory menu of the message input have an Open folder button that opens them in a new Files tab. [#34494](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34494), [#34525](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/discussions/34525) - **Terminal tab upgrades.** Terminal tabs are now named after the title that the program running in them sets, or after the running program itself with Open Terminal 0.17.8 or later on macOS and Linux, and show an icon for well-known programs such as Claude Code, Codex, Python or Node.js. When no terminal is selected for the chat, the menu for opening a new tab still offers Files, new files, terminals, the browser, the desktop and voice notes, and picking one first asks you to choose a terminal from a searchable list. - **Grouped tools and skills.** The Tools and Skills lists in the + menu of the message input are now grouped by where each entry comes from, such as the workspace, tool servers or the terminal. [#34085](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34085) - **Reply progress on tabs.** The chat tab and side chat tabs now show a spinner while a reply is being written, so you can see from another tab when an answer is still coming. - **Rename from the tab.** Double-clicking the chat tab now renames the chat, also a chat someone else made in a folder you own, and on a folder page it renames the folder. - **Share folders from their menu.** The folder menu in the sidebar and on the folder page now has a Share option for administrators and for owners allowed to share folders. - **Sub-agent permission.** Administrators can now limit sub-agents to chosen users and groups with a Sub-agents switch under Features in the default and group permissions, or with "USER_PERMISSIONS_FEATURES_SUBAGENTS", and users without it get neither the sub-agent nor the timer tool. [#34040](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34040), [#34038](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34038) - **Lighter coding agent replies.** While a coding agent writes its reply, the chat is no longer saved in full about once a second, so long agent chats write several times less to the database for each reply. [#34062](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34062) - **Labels on failed tool calls.** Tool calls that failed now keep the short label the model gave them, also in a group of calls, where one failed call used to hide every label. [#34000](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34000), [#34529](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/discussions/34529) - **Drop files into skills.** Files and folders dragged onto a skill's files in the workspace now upload into the open folder, like the upload buttons do. - **Editable calendars.** Calendars can now be renamed and given another color after they are created, from a pencil next to each calendar in the Calendar sidebar. [#34136](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34136) - **Model names in analytics.** Admin analytics now name coding agent models by agent and model for replies made from now on, and the tooltips of the session timeline show model names instead of model IDs. - **Arena model management.** The view filter in Admin Settings > Models now has an Arena Models option that lists only the arena models, and each arena model in Admin Settings > Evaluations has a Clone button that opens a new arena model filled in from it. [#34137](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34137), [#34138](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34138) - **OAuth session removals logged.** Removing a stored OAuth session because of the "OAUTH_MAX_SESSIONS_PER_USER" limit, a sign-out sent by the identity provider or a reconnected MCP server now logs a warning naming the user, the session and the reason. [#34583](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34583), [#34580](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34580) - **Accessibility improvements.** A tab moved with Alt+Shift+Arrow keys now keeps the keyboard focus, so it can be moved several places in a row. - **General improvements.** Various improvements were implemented across the application to enhance performance, stability, and security. - **Translation updates.** Translations for Azerbaijani, Basque, Bengali, Bosnian, Bulgarian, Cebuano, Croatian, Czech, Danish, Dutch, Estonian, Faroese, Filipino, Galician, Georgian, German, Greek, Hebrew, Hindi, Hungarian, Indonesian, Irish, Italian, Japanese, Kabyle, Korean, Latvian, Lithuanian, Malay, Norwegian Bokmål, Persian, Polish, Portuguese (Brazil), Punjabi, Romanian, Russian, Serbian, Slovak, Slovenian, Spanish (Spain), Swedish, Tamil, Thai, Tibetan, Turkish, Turkmen, Ukrainian, Urdu, Uyghur and Vietnamese were enhanced and expanded.  ### Fixed  - **Temporary chats leave no memories.** The background memory review now skips temporary chats, so facts from them are no longer saved as memories that show up in later chats. [#34263](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34263), [#34262](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34262) - **Whole numbers in parameters.** Whole-number fields in the advanced parameters, such as Context Length, Max Tokens and Seed, now reject a value with a decimal point, such as "32.767" typed for 32767, instead of saving it and running the model with a context of only a few tokens. [#34416](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34416) - **Busy servers with session sharing.** With "DATABASE_ENABLE_SESSION_SHARING" on, a burst of page loads no longer stalls for 30 seconds and fails with a "QueuePool limit" error, because chat lists and the inbox no longer open a second database connection for their extra reads. [#34185](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34185), [#34184](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34184) - **Uploads during knowledge syncs.** With a slow extraction engine, a large knowledge base sync no longer makes every other upload, chat attachments included, wait for an extraction before it is even saved, because files uploaded into knowledge bases now process at most half as many at a time as the server's worker threads ("THREAD_POOL_SIZE"), so other knowledge base uploads may wait their turn during a large sync. [#34379](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34379) - **Sign-in kept during provider outages.** A sign-in provider that is unreachable, rate limited or failing with a server error while a user's token is refreshed no longer deletes that user's stored sign-in, so tools and connections that use the sign-in token work again on the next call instead of only after signing out and back in. [#34585](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34585), [#34578](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34578) - **Chat keeps the newest turn.** A window that missed a turn, such as a laptop that just woke up, no longer moves the chat back to an older turn when it saves an edited message, so the newest reply stays in the conversation. [#34160](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34160), [#34126](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34126) - **Chats with looping history.** A chat whose saved messages loop back on themselves now opens and shows its messages instead of freezing the browser tab until it crashes. [#34481](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34481), [#34477](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34477) - **Summary kept through compaction.** A conversation compacted again between tool calls now keeps what its earlier summary held, and a chat compacted after such a compaction goes on from its newer summary instead of falling back to the older one and sending more than the model can take. [#34151](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34151), [#34141](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34141), [#34140](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34140) - **Steadier context compaction.** Compacting a chat by hand right after a failed reply now keeps the summary for the next message instead of losing it along with the failed reply, and automatic compaction now also counts a tool result that ended a reply, such as one too long for the model to answer, so the next message compacts the chat instead of sending it in full. [#34434](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34434) - **Fewer repeated context lists.** With native function calling, the list of available skills and the attached knowledge are no longer added to the chat again before every reply, only when they changed or after compaction, and recalled memories already in the chat are not sent again, so long chats use fewer tokens and need compaction later. [#34257](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34257) - **Tool call turns without text.** Replies in which a model called a tool without writing any text are now sent back to the model without an empty text, so proxies such as LiteLLM no longer turn them into a placeholder line that the model then starts copying into its answers. [#34265](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34265), [#34264](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34264) - **Final answers on vLLM.** When tool use stops, the request for a final answer no longer ends in a 400 error on vLLM, and Ollama models are kept from calling tools in it. [#34176](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34176), [#34169](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34169) - **Ollama proxy tool calls.** OpenAI-compatible clients that send back an assistant turn with only tool calls and no text no longer get a 400 "Field required" error from the Ollama OpenAI-compatible chat endpoint, so multi-turn tool calling works through it. [#34442](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34442), [#34441](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34441) - **Timed-out tool calls explained.** A terminal command or tool server call that runs past "AIOHTTP_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_TOOL_SERVER" now reaches the model as a timeout error instead of an empty one, so the model can tell you the call timed out. [#34119](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34119), [#34096](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34096) - **Tool calls in summaries.** Conversation summaries written with the plain summary prompt, as for models with a filter, now include the tool calls of earlier replies in saved chats. [#34139](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34139) - **MCP tools reconnect.** When an MCP server drops its connection during a reply, for example because it restarted, the next call to one of its tools now connects again instead of every later call in that reply failing with an "MCP client is not connected" error. [#34170](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34170) - **Large MCP tool lists.** MCP servers whose tool list or a single tool result is larger than 1 MB connect and answer again, also through Tether, instead of failing with "Failed to connect to MCP server". [#34421](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34421), [#34201](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34201) - **MCP sign-ins with rotating tokens.** Two tool calls that refresh the same MCP sign-in at the same time no longer delete the connection with providers that hand out a new refresh token on every refresh, because the refreshes now wait for each other, across workers when Redis is used. [#34582](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34582) - **MCP OAuth with HS256 tokens.** Connecting an MCP server over OAuth 2.1 now works when the server signs its ID token with the client secret, as Readwise does, instead of failing with "Algorithm 'HS256' requires 'oct' key". [#34593](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34593), [#34504](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34504) - **MCP OAuth with large headers.** Adding an MCP server with OAuth 2.1 and refreshing its tokens now work when its authorization server sends a very large response header, as monday.com does, instead of failing with "Connection error". [#34084](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34084), [#34078](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34078) - **Sign-in prompt for SSO tools.** When the single sign-on session whose token a tool server forwards has ended, those tools now show as needing a sign-in in the + menu and picking one takes you to sign in again, instead of the tools failing on every call with no hint. [#34579](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34579) - **OAuth sessions on other devices.** Signing in past "OAUTH_MAX_SESSIONS_PER_USER" now removes the stored provider sessions used least recently instead of the ones created first, so a device signed in early and still in use keeps its session, and signing out now removes that browser's stored session unless it is the person's only one for that provider. [#34586](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34586), [#34581](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34581) - **Quieter search logs.** Knowledge searches and several web search engines no longer write found file names or full search results to the server log at the default level, only with "GLOBAL_LOG_LEVEL" set to "DEBUG". [#34146](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34146), [#34129](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34129) - **Deleted accounts leave access lists.** Deleting an account now also removes it from the access lists of everything it was given access to, where it used to stay as a row showing only the deleted account's ID. [#34223](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34223), [#30023](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/discussions/30023) - **Local speech-to-text on pip installs.** Local speech-to-text in Open WebUI installed with pip now transcribes again instead of failing every recording with an error about "metadata_errors". [#34280](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34280), [#34278](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34278) - **OpenAI image generation defaults.** Generating images with OpenAI now works with the stock settings, because GPT image models get the auto size instead of the old 512x512 default they reject and Auto can be saved with the model field left empty, and a failed request now shows the provider's own error, such as "Invalid size". - **Fuller web page text.** With the Playwright web loader, the text of definition lists, of figures with their captions and of collapsible sections, open or closed, now reaches the model, so attached web pages and web search results no longer lose content such as the function and parameter descriptions on many documentation sites. [#34282](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34282), [#34281](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34281), [#34277](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34277), [#34272](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34272) - **Unreachable sites skipped sooner.** With SSL verification on, the Playwright, Tavily and Microsoft Web IQ web loaders now skip a site that never answers after 10 seconds instead of waiting about two minutes per link. [#34490](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34490), [#14241](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/discussions/14241) - **Web pages behind internal certificates.** With SSL verification on, the Playwright, Tavily and Microsoft Web IQ web loaders now load pages signed by an internal certificate authority set through "AIOHTTP_CLIENT_SSL_CERT_FILE" or "AIOHTTP_CLIENT_SESSION_SSL", and links that spell out a port or a username, instead of rejecting them. [#34482](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34482), [#34479](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34479), [#34462](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34462) - **No keyboard pop-up on phones.** On a phone, opening a chat, switching chats or starting a new chat no longer puts the cursor in the message input and brings up the on-screen keyboard over the conversation. [#34059](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34059) - **Smooth Firefox scrolling.** Scrolling up through a long chat in Firefox no longer throws the view back down, sometimes by several screens, while older messages load. [#33998](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33998), [#33994](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33994) - **Temporary chat choice kept.** With Temporary Chat by default turned on in the interface settings, a new chat switched to a normal chat now stays normal after a page reload. - **Permission changes reach open pages.** When an admin changes permissions, open pages now follow without a reload, so a new chat that is open becomes a temporary chat once Enforce Temporary Chat is turned on instead of failing on the first message, Compare in the model selector turns off and only the first selected model stays once Multiple Models in Chat is turned off, an open channel shows the permission notice in place of the channel once Channels is turned off, and turning off Folders removes Move from the chat menu and closes an open Create Folder window. [#34513](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34513), [#34512](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34512), [#34515](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34515), [#34514](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34514), [#34511](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34511), [#34510](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34510), [#34509](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34509), [#34508](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34508), [#34507](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34507), [#34506](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34506) - **Images reach coding agents.** Images attached to a message in a coding agent chat now reach the agent with the message instead of being dropped without a word. [#33993](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33993), [#33992](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33992), [#33991](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/discussions/33991) - **Coding agent chat titles.** Chats with a coding agent now get a generated title once the agent has replied, instead of keeping the default title. [#33971](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33971), [#33970](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33970) - **Agent model switches keep sessions.** Switching a coding agent chat to another model of the same agent now resumes the agent's existing session and working folder instead of starting a new session. - **Readable coding agent errors.** When the terminal refuses to start a coding agent, the reply now shows a short error instead of a technical dump containing the whole system prompt. [#34173](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34173), [#34172](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34172) - **Sub-agents with browser questions.** A sub-agent no longer hangs on "Executing Sub-agent" when a filter or tool asks the browser something, because the script, confirmation or form now appears in the tab that has the chat open. [#34045](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34045), [#34041](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34041) - **Sub-agent reports stay together.** Two background sub-agent reports that finish close together no longer split the chat into two branches that hide the first report and the reply to it. [#34542](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34542), [#34540](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34540) - **Shared chat changes saved.** Someone replying in a chat shared with Allow replies can now change its model, terminal and working folder, and the choice is saved to the chat instead of being dropped when it is reopened. - **Terminal choice kept in folders.** In a new chat inside a folder with a default terminal, a terminal you pick or turn off now stays that way when the model changes, instead of going back to the folder default, and a new chat in a folder without a default terminal keeps the terminal you had selected instead of turning it off. [#33973](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33973) - **Terminal locked during replies.** The terminal and working folder pickers in the message input now stay locked while a reply is still being written, so the chat's terminal can no longer be switched in the middle of a reply. [#34383](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34383) - **Steadier Tether connections.** Requests to a computer connected through Tether are no longer cut off after five minutes while data keeps flowing, read-only requests interrupted by a brief disconnect are repeated once Tether reconnects, and a busy computer now refuses new operations right away instead of letting some of them time out. - **Your devices list updates live.** A computer that connects through Tether after the page has loaded now appears under Your devices in the terminal menu right away, and the menu also picks up disconnects and device name changes, instead of needing a page reload. - **Unsaved terminal file edits kept.** An edit not yet saved in a file opened from a terminal's Files tab now comes back with its unsaved mark after you open another chat and return, and a workbook saved while you leave the chat keeps the worksheet you had open. [#34135](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34135), [#34107](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34107) - **Long Markdown files.** Opening a long Markdown file from a terminal's Files, or typing in one of its code blocks, no longer freezes the page for many seconds, because code blocks without a known language now show as plain text instead of being checked against every language, which also keeps notes and the rich text message input responsive. [#34491](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34491), [#34486](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34486) - **File cards follow the terminal.** A file shown inline in a reply now gets its preview, Download and Open back as soon as its terminal is picked again, and stops reaching the file once another terminal is picked, instead of keeping its first state until a page reload. [#34101](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34101) - **Working folder stays put.** Browsing to another folder in a Files tab no longer changes the working folder shown in the working directory menu and used by a new chat, and opening Files again shows an existing Files tab where you left it instead of moving it to the working folder. - **Terminal skill mentions after reload.** A sent message that mentions a skill of the picked terminal now still shows it as a chip after a page reload, and the + menu lists that terminal's skills right away, instead of both only working after the skill list was opened once. [#34102](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34102) - **File sizes after Refresh.** In a terminal's file viewer, Refresh and the back and forward arrows now keep the open file's real size instead of showing 0 B, and Refresh shows the new size when the file changed. [#34459](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34459), [#34458](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34458) - **Files menu near edges.** The menu from a right click on the empty part of a Files list now opens upwards or to the left when there is no room below or beside the pointer, so all of its entries stay inside the window. [#34125](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34125), [#34097](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34097) - **Local embedding model folders.** With the embedding model set to a folder on disk, saving Admin Settings > Documents no longer shows a "Model filesystem path detected" error and now also saves the embedding settings such as the batch size, which were skipped before. [#33978](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33978), [#33977](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33977) - **PowerPoint decks with sections.** PowerPoint files that use sections now preview in the chat and the knowledge base file viewer and show their comments, instead of failing with "Missing slide relationship". [#34276](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34276), [#34270](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34270) - **Large workbook sheets.** Excel workbooks with a sheet too large to preview now still open with their sheet tabs in a terminal's Files tab, the chat and the knowledge base, where that sheet says it is too large to preview and can be downloaded, instead of the whole file failing to preview. [#34247](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34247) - **Notes with footnotes.** Notes containing footnote references such as "[^1]" now open and show their content instead of failing with an error. [#33987](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33987), [#33986](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33986) - **Note recordings on S3 storage.** Recordings made in a note now upload to S3-compatible storage that rejects colons in file names and are named like "Recording-2026-11-16 15-25-06.webm" in every language, instead of after a date that slashes could cut short. [#34545](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34545), [#34543](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34543) - **Optional valves set to Custom.** Switching an optional valve, such as an on/off setting, from Default to Custom in the Valves section of the chat controls no longer fails with "Error updating function user valves", and emptying an optional on/off or number valve sets it back to its default. [#34478](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34478), [#34475](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34475) - **Math in table headers.** Formulas in table headers now keep their letters as written instead of being capitalized along with the header text. [#34245](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34245), [#34238](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34238) - **Math next to hyphens.** Inline formulas written directly next to a hyphen or non-breaking hyphen, as some models write them, now render instead of staying plain text or showing a math error. [#34244](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34244), [#34237](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34237) - **Pinning keeps chat order.** Pinning, unpinning, archiving or restoring a chat no longer moves it to the top of the chat list as if it had just been updated, and pinning a chat or moving it to a folder no longer marks it as read. - **Drafts kept per folder.** Unsent text in a new chat now stays with the folder or home page you typed it on, so opening another folder shows that folder's own unsent text instead of bringing yours along. - **Links in messages.** A link to a folder in a chat or channel message now opens the folder in Open WebUI instead of a new browser tab, and Ctrl or Cmd clicking a link to a chat, note or channel in a message now opens it in a new browser tab instead of the current one. [#34464](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34464) - **Tagging chats in parallel.** Adding the same new tag to several chats at the same time, as an API client tagging a batch of chats or two chat imports can do, no longer fails with a server error for all but one chat and leaves the others untagged. [#34144](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34144), [#34143](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34143) - **Thread reply notifications.** Clicking the browser notification for a reply in a channel thread now opens that thread instead of the channel with the thread closed. [#34233](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34233), [#34232](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34232) - **Side chats after reload.** A saved side chat tab restored after a page reload or on another device now shows only the side conversation again, instead of also listing the messages copied from the parent chat. [#34002](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34002) - **Browser tab in two windows.** With the same chat open in two windows, a Browser tab in Chrome mode no longer keeps reconnecting in both, and the window that lost the tab shows that the browser opened in another viewer. [#34133](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34133), [#34095](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34095) - **Ports open in new tabs.** The Open in new tab button beside a port on the start page of a Browser tab now opens the server running on that port instead of a "404: Not Found" page. [#34120](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34120), [#34108](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34108) - **Smooth Browser tab resizing.** Dragging the divider of the side panel no longer makes the Browser tab flicker and resize the page in slow motion. [#33989](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33989), [#33988](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33988) - **Terminal proxy headers.** A "TERMINAL_PROXY_HEADERS" entry written in a different letter case now replaces the terminal's own header instead of being sent next to it, and repeated headers from the terminal such as several cookies are all passed on. [#34092](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34092) - **Groups need a name.** A new group can no longer be saved without a name from a tab other than General, because Save stays off until a name is typed and the server now rejects empty or blank group names. [#34518](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34518) - **User list API groups.** The admin endpoint that lists all users now includes each user's groups instead of an empty list. [#34489](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34489), [#26156](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/discussions/26156) - **Model filter tags update.** The tag filter of the model selector now also lists tags that models gain while a chat is open, instead of only the tags present when the page loaded. [#33990](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33990), [#33806](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33806) - **Mark folder chats read.** Mark all as read in the menu on a folder page now marks the folder's chats as read instead of doing nothing. [#34404](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34404), [#34403](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34403) - **Folder page kept.** Opening a chat in another browser tab no longer sends a folder's chat list back to page 1. [#34145](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34145), [#34142](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34142) - **Same-folder chat drops.** Dropping a chat back on the folder it is already in no longer moves it there again and reloads every folder in the sidebar. [#34461](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34461), [#34460](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34460) - **Comment box on Android.** Writing a comment on selected text on an Android phone no longer closes the comment box as soon as the keyboard opens. - **Steadier menu placement.** Menus that open above their button, such as Model controls and the + menu of the message input, now grow upward when their content changes instead of briefly covering the message input, and right-aligned menus in Firefox no longer creep sideways while they open. [#34052](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34052), [#34051](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34051), [#34227](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34227), [#34226](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34226) - **Long settings text boxes.** Long prompt templates and JSON fields in Admin Settings now stop growing at a set height and scroll, instead of stretching the page to fit all of their text. [#34569](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34569) - **Hover preview behind menus.** A chat's hover preview in the sidebar no longer opens on top of another chat's open menu and covers its items. [#34231](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34231), [#34230](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34230) - **Hover preview without bubbles.** With Chat Bubble UI turned off, your messages in a chat's hover preview now show as padded bubbles instead of text squeezed onto a grey block. [#34347](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34347) - **Translated automation schedules.** The Schedule button in the dialog that creates an automation now shows the schedule in the chosen language instead of always in English. [#34428](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34428), [#34426](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34426) - **Qdrant version warning.** Connecting to a Qdrant 1.20 server, as the vector database or for external Qdrant knowledge bases, no longer logs a client version warning, which servers on Qdrant 1.18 or older now get instead. [#34485](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34485), [#34483](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34483) - **Image zoom reset.** Resetting the zoom of an image preview from below 10% now returns it to 100% and centers it again. [#34080](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34080), [#34079](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34079) - **Steady Tools count.** The count on the Tools tab of the workspace no longer drops to 0 for a moment when the Tools page opens. [#34236](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34236) - **Unpin tooltip on notes.** The Unpin button on a pinned note in the sidebar now shows a tooltip, as it does on pinned models. [#34568](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34568), [#34567](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34567)  ### Changed  - **Terminals 0.4.0 required.** Connections to a Terminals server no longer carry a policy in Admin Settings > Integrations, so the Policy ID, image, resource, storage, idle timeout and Terminal Contexts settings and the Refresh Terminals button are gone, the server has to run Terminals 0.4.0 or later, and models, folders, automations and chats that used the old connection need one of the new terminals selected. Upgrading Terminals from 0.3.x takes its manual migration, as there is no automatic one, new Terminals installations start without a template, Open WebUI now connects with the Terminals server key, and "TERMINALS_OPEN_WEBUI_URL" and "TERMINALS_TOKEN_CACHE_TTL" should be removed from the Terminals settings. Open Terminal and Tether terminal connections are not affected. - **Direct connections move to Tether.** Settings > Connections and Settings > Tools are gone together with the Direct Connections and Direct Integrations switches in Admin Settings, the Direct Tool Servers permission and the "ENABLE_DIRECT_CONNECTIONS", "ENABLE_DIRECT_INTEGRATIONS" and "USER_PERMISSIONS_FEATURES_DIRECT_TOOL_SERVERS" settings, so models, tool servers and terminals that users added in their own settings no longer appear. Users add them in the Tether app (0.1.14 or later, 0.1.16 for MCP servers) instead, where they stay on their own computer and work in every Open WebUI they connect. - **Image settings become connections.** On the first start of this version, the image generation and editing settings are turned into image connections that everyone who could use them before keeps using, and from then on the image environment variables such as "IMAGE_GENERATION_ENGINE", "IMAGES_OPENAI_API_BASE_URL" and "COMFYUI_BASE_URL" are no longer read unless "ENABLE_PERSISTENT_CONFIG" is turned off. - **Locked Docker dependencies.** The standard and slim Docker images now install every Python package except PyTorch from lock files pinned with hashes, so rebuilding the same version installs the same packages, and custom images that edit "backend/requirements.txt" must regenerate the locks with "backend/update-locks.sh" for the edit to take effect. [#33981](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/33981), [#33979](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/33979) - **New coding agent model IDs.** Coding agent models now get IDs made only of letters, digits, dashes and underscores, which makes pinned Cursor and Pi models work, and pins, default models and automations that point at an agent model whose name had other characters, dots included as in most Codex models, need the model picked again. [#34171](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34171), [#34168](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/issues/34168) - **Coding agent sessions restart once.** The first new message in a coding agent chat from an earlier version starts a new agent session that gets the chat so far as text, instead of resuming the agent's own session. - **Image API model checks.** Image generation and edit requests sent through the API now run on the model they name, which has to be one of the caller's image models or the request fails with an error, instead of image generation ignoring the named model. - **Live update events renamed.** Real-time updates for notes, comments, the inbox, access changes, encrypted channels and channel app errors now arrive as typed messages on the main "events" socket channel, so custom clients that listened for the old event names such as "events:note" or "note:presence" need to listen there instead. - **Chat write access field.** Chat responses from the API now carry the saved sharing mode in "share_mode" and report whether you can reply in a new "write_access" field, so custom clients that read "share_mode" for that need to switch. - **Terminal uploads go to files.** New terminal connections now start with Chat Uploads set to Filesystem and computers connected through Tether always use it, so files attached in a chat are saved into the selected terminal's working folder instead of being processed as regular chat uploads, while existing connections keep their setting. Open Terminal 0.17.20 or later is recommended, as it fixes uploads becoming unreadable in multi-user setups. - **Chat menu items moved.** Overview, Artifacts and Comments are no longer in the chat's menu and now open from the + menu of the tab bar, where Overview opens as a tab beside the chat instead of a full-screen window. - **Note comments button.** Comments on a note now open from a button in the note's top bar instead of from its menu, and in a note you can edit, commenting on selected text is now a Comment button in the formatting toolbar. - **One heading menu.** The formatting toolbar of the message input and of notes now offers Heading 1, 2 and 3 in a single heading menu instead of three separate buttons. - **Attachments menu renamed.** The Add Context entry in the + menu of the message input, which holds the options to attach web pages, files, notes, knowledge and chats, is now called Attachments. [#34431](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34431) - **Capabilities now Features.** The Capabilities section of the model editor is now called Features. [#34433](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34433) - **Knowledge base labels.** The knowledge picker in the chat, the knowledge selector of the model editor and attached knowledge bases now call them Knowledge or Knowledge Base instead of Collection, the vector database term, and the search box inside a knowledge base now says Search Files. [#34432](https://github.com/open-webui/open-webui/pull/34432) 
```

### 2026-11-11T22:47:59Z [**gitbutlerapp/gitbutler**](https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/gitbutler)

```
## Changes  - Repositories using Git LFS or git-crypt no longer get a warning to avoid GitButler; it now only covers editing and resolving commits  ## Fixes  - Linux: `.deb` and `.rpm` installs now send you to the downloads page to update instead of failing - GitHub: Auto-merge now works in repositories that only allow squash or rebase merges - Fixes AI requests failing with `401 Unauthorized` when using Anthropic with GitButler's key  ## CLI  - `but apply`, `but squash`, `but show` and `but pr` now suggest a next step when they refuse  ## Native app  Highlights from the native app. New installs now open it by default; everyone else can turn it on (or off) under "Experimental" in settings:  - The Graph tab now shows a commit's diff, with keyboard navigation and right-click actions - "New terminal with…" (`⌘I`) opens a terminal running a command you pick, e.g. `claude` or `codex` - Appearance settings now let you pick a preset, accent color, corner style and density - Files and hunks in a branch's diff now support the same actions as in a commit's diff - Adds "Recycle worktree" to reuse a clean archived worktree instead of creating a new one - Stacks now sit under a caption that folds them and can push, merge or unapply the whole stack - `⇧⌘P` now opens the command palette instead of the Create pull request form - A plain `git commit` on `gitbutler/workspace` no longer breaks the project; the app now offers to uncommit it and keep the changes - Projects whose folder was deleted or moved now say so and can be removed from Settings  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/gitbutler/compare/release/0.23.3...release/0.23.4  ## Downloads  Download bins from https://gitbutler.com/ 
```

# 電視版 5.8.8  * 調整爬蟲對話框樣式。 * 支援爬蟲更新首頁分類。 * 新增下一集解碼預熱選項。 * 修正投放進度與播放狀態還原。 * 支援爬蟲指定播放清單中的集數。 * 緩衝開始前不顯示遙控器定位預覽。 * 新增 ExoPlayer 與 MPV 畫面補幀。 * 請至「設定 → 模組」更新 QuickJS 與 MPV。  # 手機版 5.8.8  * 簡化雙指縮放操作。 * 調整爬蟲對話框樣式。 * 支援爬蟲更新首頁分類。 * 新增下一集解碼預熱選項。 * 修正投放進度與播放狀態還原。 * 支援爬蟲指定播放清單中的集數。 * 新增 ExoPlayer 與 MPV 畫面補幀。 * 請至「設定 → 模組」更新 QuickJS 與 MPV。

install homebrew-ffmpeg/ffmpeg/ffmpeg        # Ubuntu/Debian    sudo apt-get install ffmpeg libsndfile1-dev      3. 安装 uv：    bash    curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/install.sh | sh      4. ### 2026-11-11T09:07:31Z [**lollipopkit/flutter_server_box**](https://github.com/lollipopkit/flutter_server_box)

```
### Before you upgrade  - **Usage analytics are removed**, along with the Full diagnostics level. Installations set to Full now use Basic, which uploads only errors and crashes. - **Monitor v0.4.0 uses a single account and signs in with a password only.** Signing in grants the permissions of the system account Monitor runs as; roles, per-feature permissions, file roots and read-only mode are removed. On upgrade, the oldest admin account is kept and other accounts' tasks, memory and desk data move to it. ServerBox no longer asks for a username, and offers to update Monitors older than v0.4.0 before signing in. - **Self-signed Monitor certificates are trusted once by fingerprint.** The "ignore certificate" toggle is replaced by SHA-256 fingerprint pinning: the first connection prompts you to trust the certificate from the server card or detail page, and prompts again only if the certificate changes. - **Sync uses a new incremental format.** iCloud and WebDAV now sync individual changes instead of full backups, encrypted with a sync key protected by your backup password. GitHub Gist and Monitor's hosted backup store are removed as sync destinations. All devices sharing a destination must use the same backup password and upgrade to this version; older versions cannot read changes written by newer ones. - **The Monitor panel's terminal no longer connects over SSH.** It opens a local shell directly under the system account Monitor runs as. - New Monitor panel features, the Monitor tab's management views, and the new notification channels require **Monitor v0.4.0**.  ### Added  - **Storage and sharing** in the app and the Monitor panel: manage disks, partitions, RAID arrays, ZFS pools, LUKS encryption and SMART health, plus SMB shares (with Time Machine and recycle bin options) and NFS exports. - **Monitor panel redesigned as a desktop:** every feature opens in its own window, with a menu bar, dock, Spotlight search, notification center, tabs and split panes in Terminal and Files, and customizable wallpapers and themes. - **Expanded server tools in the Monitor panel:** processes, services, scheduled tasks, containers (images, logs, container shell, and scheduled volume and folder backups), system users and groups, snippets, firewall, benchmark, tmux, iperf, file editor, remote desktop (VNC and RDP), virtualization and BMC (Redfish). - **App Store for self-hosted web apps:** deploy and update over 40 Docker Compose services from the Monitor panel (including Immich, Jellyfin, Nextcloud, Vaultwarden, Home Assistant, Gitea, Open WebUI, Grafana and n8n), or load custom catalogs and local Compose apps, with port checks, health alerts, a shared media folder and built-in reverse proxy access. - **Desk apps (`.fsba`) in the Monitor panel:** install sandboxed third-party panel apps with approved permissions, alongside signed official packages for **Local inference** (Ollama, llama.cpp, vLLM, SGLang and MLX matched to the machine's GPU), **AI agents** (Codex, Claude Code, pi, opencode, Hermes and OpenClaw), **Reverse proxy and WAF** (nginx, Caddy, Traefik, and ModSecurity with OWASP CRS), **Tailscale**, **Kernels**, **Ubuntu Pro** and **Cleanup**. - **Agent mode on Monitor, integrated with the app:** run AI tasks on Linux, macOS and Windows servers with per-task model, thinking level and permission mode, execution plans, command risk checks, sudo prompts, attachments, memory, MCP servers (with OAuth and deployed apps' MCP servers) and skills. Tasks can also be started, followed live and answered from the app's Agent tab. - **Agent command permissions:** choose Manual, Auto or Allow all mode and configure allow, ask or deny command rules for both the on-device Agent and Monitor's Agent mode. - **Monitor tab, one-click updates and settings backup:** view all connected Monitors at a glance—version status and one-click updates, active alert rules, deployed web apps (with available updates listed first) opened through Monitor's proxy, notifications, per-server audit logs, and signed-in sessions and trusted devices that can be revoked. Each server page also lists its own web apps, and Monitor can back up its settings to WebDAV as `age`-encrypted archives. - **ServerBox OS (`sbOS`) bootable images:** live and disk-installable images based on Alpine Linux (`musl`) and Debian 13 (`GNU`) for `x64` (BIOS and UEFI) and `arm64` (UEFI), with Monitor and Docker preconfigured. - **Server discovery:** find SSH servers on the local network, in SSH config files, on Tailscale and through Cloudflare Tunnel, and add them along with their jump servers. First-run setup is redesigned around it. - **Selective sync and full user-data coverage:** toggle sync separately for settings, AI preferences, credentials, key passphrases, files and benchmarks; fonts, images, skills, theme packages and icon packs now sync as well. The redesigned sync page shows each destination's live status. - **Trusted sign-in devices on Monitor:** devices that have signed in before receive a device token, so failed sign-in attempts from other sources do not lock them out. - **New notification channels:** SMTP email, Telegram, Discord and ntfy, plus `${ENV}` variable references for push credentials in `config.toml`. Bark adds subtitle, Markdown, critical alert volume, badge, image, copy, archive options and AES-CBC encrypted push. - **GPU process list** for servers connected through Monitor. - **iOS:** device files and installed Linux environments appear in the Files app, and Paste from the system menu uses bracketed paste in the terminal. - **Windows installer (`.exe`):** per-user installer that requires no administrator rights and bundles the Visual C++ runtime, alongside WinGet support. The portable `.zip` archive remains available. - **tmux:** zoom and unzoom panes, keeping zoom active when switching panes. - **Redesigned full-screen server view** across all platforms, showing a single server or all servers with burn-in protection.  ### Changed  - Exported backups now use the `age` format and can be decrypted with `age -d` using your backup password; older backups still restore. On Monitor, stored credentials and Agent mode chats are now encrypted at rest. - Terminal and file tabs are no longer reopened automatically after closing the app. - Improved mouse wheel and trackpad scrolling in the terminal, with whole-line wheel steps, smooth trackpad scrolling in full-screen programs and a single scrollbar. - Notification channel editors now show every option a channel type supports, including unset fields. - Percentage chart axes no longer extend past 100%. - Completed and polished translations across all supported languages in the app, Monitor panel and documentation.  ### Fixed  - macOS: fixed garbled text on Intel Macs (#1798) by switching the Intel DMG build to the Skia renderer. - Database: fixed a migration failure when opening databases from older releases (prior to schema v31), and added retries for transient I/O errors when opening the database on Windows. - tmux: fixed pane and window target resolution, cross-window switching, clean detaching, live screen tracking on pane switches, and output replay after reconnecting. - Terminal: typing quickly no longer duplicates characters; opening a session from the picker switches to its tab; replacing the background image takes effect immediately; and the Files button resolves the shell's working directory without typing commands into the shell. - Editor: copying a large selection while an input method is active no longer drops lines. - Settings and navigation: pages with unsaved edits ask before closing, and Back navigation returns to the visible page across tabs, workspace panes and wide windows. - Dialog buttons remain responsive after the window is resized or the screen rotates. - Mobile: fixed Paste in text fields and the terminal on iOS, merged foreground session notifications, and fixed keyboard bounce when switching views. - Remote desktop now shows a clear explanation when a server supports only Standard RDP Security. - Monitor: Linux builds start on CPUs without AVX; NVIDIA GPU processes are listed again when the driver does not report per-process memory (Windows and WSL); notification channel errors no longer expose request URLs that could contain tokens; and newly installed Monitors no longer send alerts to blank placeholder channels.  中文更新日志：https://serverbox.lollipopkit.com/docs/zh/changelog/  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/lollipopkit/flutter_server_box/compare/v1.0.1856...v1.0.1925 
```

### 2026-11-12T14:55:04Z [**mholt/caddy-l4**](https://github.com/mholt/caddy-l4)

```
This release changes how UDP and other packet connections are served: they are now split into a flow per client. It also adds timeouts that limit how long stalled TLS handshakes and half-closed proxied connections can last. Beyond that, it fixes a number of crashes, leaks and hangs in matching, `proxy`, `proxy_protocol` and `remote_ip_list`, and bumps dependencies.  ## Highlights  - **UDP flows now go through [vnxme/hybrid](https://github.com/vnxme/hybrid).** Packet connections such as UDP sockets are served as a wrapper. Each client (downstream `address:port`) gets its own flow, and a flow ends when its connection is closed or goes idle (#485, #494). - **New timeouts to stop stalled connections from hanging around:**   - `tls` handler: `handshake_timeout` limits how long the TLS handshake may take (#470)   - `proxy` handler: `half_close_timeout` closes a half-closed connection after it has been idle that long. Every read resets the timer, so data still flowing in the open direction is never cut (#468)    Both default to `0`, which keeps the old behavior. - **Requires Go 1.26 and Caddy v2.11.7** (#474, #476)  ## Fixes  ### Core / matching - A route that conclusively failed to match no longer stops matching early. Previously, e.g. an `http` route placed after a `tls` route could make matching give up within microseconds, long before `matching_timeout` (#455) - Packet conns: fixed reads of empty and large packets, and of packets only partly prefetched. Sub-second deadlines also work now (#484) - UDP session goroutines and their buffers are released when the server shuts down or reloads. Before, they leaked on every reload (#482) - Listener wrapper: the prefetch buffer of a hijacked connection is no longer returned to the pool while it is still in use. Under concurrency this could hand one client's ClientHello to another connection ("server did not echo the legacy session ID") (#483)  ### Handlers & matchers - `proxy`: fixed a nil dereference that crashed the process when sending a PROXY header to upstreams whose addresses contain placeholders (#486) - `proxy`: half-closing a TLS connection now sends a TCP FIN after `close_notify`, so TCP-only peers (e.g. L4 load balancers) see the connection end (#466) - `proxy`: active health checks keep the upstream address (#458) - `proxy_protocol`: the computed policy is now passed to go-proxyproto. With go-proxyproto ≥ v0.15.0, headerless connections allowed by `USE`/`IGNORE` fallbacks were being rejected, and `IGNORE` did not actually ignore the header (#475) - `remote_ip_list`: `Cleanup` no longer blocks after the context is cancelled. Previously this hung config reloads and the admin API (#492) - `clock`: a panic guard was added, and `after` > `before` is now treated as an overnight window that wraps past midnight (#449)  ## Docs & tests - Documented HTTP/2 connection coalescing with listener wrappers (#480) - Added `close` handler tests and documented that the handler is terminal (#454)  ## Maintenance - Dependency and CI updates (#456, #463, #487, #488, #489)  ## New contributors Thanks to everyone who made their first contribution in this release: - @barto95100 (#482) - @cruizba (#466, #468, #470) - @cyberb (#486) - @kwuite (#492) - @lucastsudaka (#458) - @Pizmovc (#455)  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/mholt/caddy-l4/compare/v0.1.2...v0.1.3 
```

<div align="center"> <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tw93/Pake/main/assets/logo.png" alt="Pake Logo" width="120" height="120" style="border-radius:50%" /> <h1 style="margin: 12px 0 6px;">Pake V3.19.0</h1> <p><em>Turn any webpage into a desktop app with one command.</em></p> </div>  ### Changelog  1. On macOS, `--icon` now takes an Icon Composer `.icon` package, so the app icon follows the Light, Dark and Tinted appearance on macOS 26 and later, with an `.icns` for older systems. This needs Xcode 26 or later on the build machine; without it the app uses the `.icns`. 2. On Windows and Linux, Ctrl+W closes the window, Ctrl+Q quits, Ctrl+L copies the page address, and Alt+Left / Alt+Right go back and forward. A page that uses one of these keys itself still gets it. 3. On Linux, call and voice sites can use the camera and microphone after you allow it once for that site. 4. A page that fails to load on Linux shows the site and a Try Again button instead of the engine's bare error, and a page whose renderer crashes reloads by itself on Linux and Windows. 5. On Windows, links opened from inside an embedded frame, such as a Power Apps `Launch()` button, open in the system browser. 6. After a download finishes on macOS and Linux, clicking the message shows the file in its folder. 7. On macOS, an app with a transparent title bar opens in the page's last color instead of flashing white, a window preset taller than the screen fits on it, and leaving full screen returns to the configured size. 8. With find enabled, sites that have their own search, such as Notion and Google Docs, keep Ctrl+F on Windows and Linux. 9. Pressing Ctrl+C while the CLI is downloading an icon stops the build right away instead of waiting for the 10-second download timeout.  ### 更新日志  1. macOS 上 `--icon` 现在可以传 Icon Composer 的 `.icon` 包，应用图标在 macOS 26 及以上会跟着浅色、深色和着色外观变化，旧系统用 `.icns`，打包机器需要 Xcode 26 或更高版本，没有的话就用 `.icns`。 2. Windows 和 Linux 上 Ctrl+W 关闭窗口、Ctrl+Q 退出、Ctrl+L 复制网页地址，Alt+左右方向键前进后退，网页自己用到这些按键时照旧交给网页。 3. Linux 上通话和语音类网站在你允许一次之后，就可以用摄像头和麦克风了。 4. Linux 上网页加载失败时会显示网站名和重试按钮，不再是引擎的报错原文，Linux 和 Windows 上网页渲染进程崩溃后会自己重新加载。 5. Windows 上从网页内嵌框架里打开的链接，比如 Power Apps 里 `Launch()` 按钮打开的链接，现在会在系统浏览器里打开。 6. macOS 和 Linux 上下载完成后，点一下提示就能在文件夹里找到这个文件。 7. macOS 上透明标题栏的应用打开时直接用网页上次的颜色，不再先闪一下白色，比屏幕还高的窗口预设会缩到屏幕内，退出全屏后也会回到设定的尺寸。 8. 开启查找后，Notion、Google Docs 这类自带搜索的网站在 Windows 和 Linux 上能继续用自己的 Ctrl+F。 9. CLI 下载图标时按 Ctrl+C 会立刻停止构建，不用再等 10 秒的下载超时。  Special thanks to @crispyaus, @yuxino, and @adamsiwiec1 for the pull requests behind this release. If Pake helps you, please consider giving it a star and recommending it to your friends.  > https://github.com/tw93/Pake

(#5632, PR #5819 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55))  🐛修复 - 修复番剧播放页样式有时会失效的问题. (#4250, PR #5830 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复设置面板内下拉菜单被滚动容器裁切的问题. (PR #5827 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复在线功能导入、搜索和提示框的问题, 并在安装时拒绝缺少必要字段的组件、插件和样式. (PR #5834 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55), PR #5835 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复 `传统连播模式` 在新版多 P 和合集页面失效的问题. (#5843, PR #5847 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复 `滚轮调节音量` 拦截播放器控制栏和浮层上的滚动操作. (PR #5842 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - `直播首页静音` 和 `直播首页暂停` 共用隐藏直播首页板块的样式. (PR #5836 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复 `快捷键扩展` 加载已失效的动作绑定时出错的问题. (#5863, PR #5871 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - 修复 `自定义顶栏` 个人信息面板的大会员权益到期时间显示. (PR #5868 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55)) - `下载视频 - Motrix 输出支持` 适配 Motrix 2 的链接协议, 多分段地址通过剪贴板传入新建任务. (#5860, PR #5865 by [司城 ユヅキ](https://github.com/WhiteTeal55))  ☕开发者相关 - DevClient 不再将临时开发地址写入到脚本存储, 改为 sessionStorage 实现, 避免开发地址残留的问题.  ----  ⚠ 请勿直接下载下方的压缩包进行安装, 只有[首页](https://github.com/the1812/Bilibili-Evolved#%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85)的安装链接是正确的安装方式.
